## 约好更换弯曲的自行车前轮 · p07-a-01

主题：消费与服务；情景：询问自行车维修安排（bicycle-repair-estimate）。

沟通目标：检查车况、讨论维修必要性并确认取车时间。

背景：骑车人推着车到维修店，想确认车轮是否需要更换及何时能取车。

人物：Nia、Owen。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-01-s01 · Nia: The front wheel looks bent. Can it be repaired?
- p07-a-01-s02 · Owen: Let me check it on the stand. No, this rim needs replacing; I wouldn't ride on it.
- p07-a-01-s03 · Nia: Can you replace it today? I can leave the bike here.
- p07-a-01-s04 · Owen: Yes. The total would be forty pounds, and you could collect it at five.
- p07-a-01-s05 · Nia: That works. Please replace it, and I'll come back at five.

目标用法：

- bent · adj. · 弯曲变形的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ccae6da9b110b168-p07-a-01；来源 ox-ccae6da9b110b168；证据 p07-a-01-s01；词形 bent。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The rider asks whether the damaged front wheel can be saved, the mechanic identifies the rim as needing replacement, and the rider authorizes the quoted repair and five o'clock collection. The reused repair-arrangements goal is achieved; the brief exchange is coherent and the title accurately describes the agreement rather than completed repair.

绑定文件 SHA-256：40818cc60df5a252e271b56338f7526fe5d6f2ba2630d648c0b60bb365df68f2

- ox-ccae6da9b110b168：accepted；In s01, looks bent predicates an adjective of the visibly deformed front wheel. The mechanic's finding that its rim needs replacing confirms physical deformation, not a figurative inclination; the Chinese sense fits.（p07-a-01-s01）。

<a id="dialogue-p07-a-02"></a>

## 取消林间木屋预订并确认退款 · p07-a-02

主题：出行与住宿；情景：取消木屋住宿并核对退款（cabin-reservation-cancellation）。

沟通目标：确认要取消的木屋住宿、取消条件和退款安排，完成取消并收到确认

背景：旅客因家庭安排有变，致电虚构的度假营地取消尚在免费取消期内的住宿。

人物：Tomas、Rae。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-02-s01 · Tomas: I'd like to cancel my booking for next weekend. The name is Tomas Vale.
- p07-a-02-s02 · Rae: I have it here: the two-person cabin for Friday and Saturday. Is that the right one?
- p07-a-02-s03 · Tomas: Yes. Our family circumstances have changed, and we can't travel now.
- p07-a-02-s04 · Rae: I'm sorry to hear that. You're still within our free cancellation period, so the full deposit can be returned.
- p07-a-02-s05 · Tomas: Thank you. Please go ahead.
- p07-a-02-s06 · Rae: It's cancelled now. I've sent the confirmation, and the refund should reach the same card within five working days.
- p07-a-02-s07 · Tomas: The email has arrived. Thanks for making that straightforward.

目标用法：

- booking · n. · 住宿预订；义项 sense-staged-ox-a427ea8799a538ba-p07-a-02；来源 ox-a427ea8799a538ba；证据 p07-a-02-s01；词形 booking。
- cancel · v. · 取消；义项 sense-staged-ox-3b4667e423ef1044-p07-a-02；来源 ox-3b4667e423ef1044；证据 p07-a-02-s01；词形 cancel。
- cabin · n. · 度假住宿用的小木屋；义项 sense-staged-ox-24b1bc0ccdfa132c-p07-a-02；来源 ox-24b1bc0ccdfa132c；证据 p07-a-02-s02；词形 cabin。
- circumstance · n. · 影响安排的家庭情况；义项 sense-staged-ox-1a2a4121da982ed9-p07-a-02；来源 ox-1a2a4121da982ed9；证据 p07-a-02-s03；词形 circumstances。
  - 词形依据：使用名词的复数形式 circumstances，表示家庭处境和条件。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The caller identifies the reservation, confirms its dates and cabin, gives a restrained reason, authorizes cancellation after hearing the terms, and acknowledges the confirmation email. The refund is correctly presented as pending rather than received; the cancellation-and-refund-arrangements goal and Chinese title fit the exact ending.

绑定文件 SHA-256：bc467d2ab07f6aff31c67388b4d113435baa9295d4aa24928faa885294028bc6

- ox-a427ea8799a538ba：accepted；In s01, my booking is the noun object of cancel; the next turn identifies it as the two-night cabin reservation, supporting the accommodation sense rather than a sporting penalty.（p07-a-02-s01）。
- ox-3b4667e423ef1044：accepted；In s01, cancel follows would like to and takes my booking as its object. Later authorization and confirmation establish the intended cancellation action, so the base verb and Chinese gloss are exact.（p07-a-02-s01）。
- ox-24b1bc0ccdfa132c：accepted；In s02, the two-person cabin names the small holiday accommodation attached to the reservation, not an aircraft compartment. Singular count-noun syntax and the holiday-camp context fit the selected sense.（p07-a-02-s02）。
- ox-1a2a4121da982ed9：accepted；In s03, family circumstances is the plural noun subject of have changed, naturally expressing personal conditions that now prevent travel. The declared plural form and its rationale match that collective use.（p07-a-02-s03）。

<a id="dialogue-p07-a-03"></a>

## 选定沿运河到老桥的散步路线 · p07-a-03

主题：出行与住宿；情景：选择运河散步路线（canal-walk-route-choice）。

沟通目标：问清运河步道、老桥与公共通行边界，并选定往返路线

背景：两位朋友想从游客中心出发散步，请工作人员说明路线。

人物：Amir、Liv。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-03-s01 · Amir: We have about an hour. Is there a pleasant walk from here?
- p07-a-03-s02 · Liv: Follow the path beside the canal. It's flat all the way to the old bridge.
- p07-a-03-s03 · Amir: What's beyond the bridge? The map seems to stop there.
- p07-a-03-s04 · Liv: There's a charming row of old cottages, but the public path ends just before their gardens.
- p07-a-03-s05 · Amir: Then we'll walk to the bridge, look at the cottages from there, and come back.
- p07-a-03-s06 · Liv: That should fit your time. Use this gate to reach the path.

目标用法：

- beside · prep. · 在……旁边；义项 sense-staged-ox-c7c4fb473afc2525-p07-a-03；来源 ox-c7c4fb473afc2525；证据 p07-a-03-s02；词形 beside。
- canal · n. · 运河；义项 sense-staged-ox-d07335065f8a05c5-p07-a-03；来源 ox-d07335065f8a05c5；证据 p07-a-03-s02；词形 canal。
- beyond · prep. · 在……更远的一边；义项 sense-staged-ox-2347c4c26fbe1e75-p07-a-03；来源 ox-2347c4c26fbe1e75；证据 p07-a-03-s03；词形 beyond。
- charming · adj. · 迷人而有吸引力的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c59bcd4cca8b6f7c-p07-a-03；来源 ox-c59bcd4cca8b6f7c；证据 p07-a-03-s04；词形 charming。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The visitor asks for an hour-long walk, establishes the canal route and its public-access boundary, then chooses the bridge as a turnaround point. Liv consistently supplies staff guidance; the other friend can remain offstage. The route-selection title and goal are achieved without implying the walk has occurred.

- ox-c7c4fb473afc2525：accepted；In s02, beside takes the canal as its complement and locates the path alongside the waterway. This is a straightforward spatial preposition rather than the additive besides.（p07-a-03-s02）。
- ox-d07335065f8a05c5：accepted；In s02, the canal is the waterway next to the level path; the subsequent bridge and route map sustain the literal count-noun reading 运河.（p07-a-03-s02）。
- ox-2347c4c26fbe1e75：accepted；In s03, beyond the bridge asks what lies on its farther side. The explicit noun-phrase complement establishes the prepositional source record rather than the available adverb record.（p07-a-03-s03）。
- ox-c59bcd4cca8b6f7c：accepted；In s04, charming modifies the row of old cottages as attractively picturesque. This evaluative adjective fits both the spoken route recommendation and the Chinese appeal/attractiveness gloss.（p07-a-03-s04）。

## 约定从开放观景台观察蝙蝠 · p07-a-04

主题：自然与环境；情景：确认保护区蝙蝠观赏位置（bat-reserve-viewing-plan）。

沟通目标：了解洞穴与悬崖步道的关闭限制，决定随导览前往开放观景台

背景：游客询问保护区工作人员，如何在不干扰蝙蝠的情况下观看它们离洞。

人物：Suri、Ellis。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-04-s01 · Suri: Is the cave open to visitors? We'd like to see the bats.
- p07-a-04-s02 · Ellis: No. A large colony lives inside, and keeping people out is part of our conservation work.
- p07-a-04-s03 · Suri: Can we watch from the path along the cliff instead?
- p07-a-04-s04 · Ellis: That path is closed too, but the lower viewing platform is open. You can see the entrance from there.
- p07-a-04-s05 · Suri: Do we need to climb down to it?
- p07-a-04-s06 · Ellis: No, there's a marked level path from the visitor centre. The evening guide will lead a group at seven.
- p07-a-04-s07 · Suri: We'll join that group and stay on the platform.

目标用法：

- cave · n. · 洞穴；义项 sense-staged-ox-1214a03d616de281-p07-a-04；来源 ox-1214a03d616de281；证据 p07-a-04-s01；词形 cave。
- colony · n. · 同处一地的动物群体；义项 sense-staged-ox-335324b18eebf955-p07-a-04；来源 ox-335324b18eebf955；证据 p07-a-04-s02；词形 colony。
- conservation · n. · 自然保护；义项 sense-staged-ox-8db2f130951d1a4f-p07-a-04；来源 ox-8db2f130951d1a4f；证据 p07-a-04-s02；词形 conservation。
- cliff · n. · 悬崖；义项 sense-staged-ox-c95c538162104c55-p07-a-04；来源 ox-c95c538162104c55；证据 p07-a-04-s03；词形 cliff。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The staff member rules out the cave and cliff path, supplies an open viewing platform and level access, and the visitor commits to the seven o'clock guided group. No claim of entering the protected area or already seeing bats is made. The nature topic, restrictions and planned viewing outcome align precisely.

绑定文件 SHA-256：8c7f9d5403989c929f9c29e6790d8af75c0150ae5119dafff6103fec52a10713

- ox-1214a03d616de281：accepted；In s01, the cave is the physical underground opening whose interior houses bats. The definite count noun is motivated by the reserve location and supports 洞穴.（p07-a-04-s01）。
- ox-335324b18eebf955：accepted；In s02, a large colony lives inside refers back to the bats in s01. The living animal group, not a settlement or political territory, is unambiguous in full context.（p07-a-04-s02）。
- ox-8db2f130951d1a4f：accepted；In s02, conservation modifies work as a noun denoting nature protection; excluding visitors protects the bat colony. That concrete purpose supports 自然保护 rather than an unrelated preservation sense.（p07-a-04-s02）。
- ox-c95c538162104c55：accepted；In s03, the cliff is the steep natural feature along which the proposed path runs. The noun after along the is spatially coherent with the lower viewing platform and fits 悬崖.（p07-a-04-s03）。

## 先拍摄园中昆虫再请专家辨认 · p07-a-05

主题：自然与环境；情景：记录待辨认的花园昆虫（garden-insect-photo-identification）。

沟通目标：描述昆虫可见特征，决定暂记为未辨认昆虫并补充原位照片和地点

背景：志愿者在社区花园做生物调查记录，遇到无法确定种类的昆虫。

人物：Mae、Dev。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-05-s01 · Mae: There's a tiny bug on that bush. Should I put it on the survey sheet?
- p07-a-05-s02 · Dev: Yes, but don't guess its name. This is a biological survey, so an uncertain record needs to be marked as uncertain.
- p07-a-05-s03 · Mae: If I look closely, I can see two pale spots on its back.
- p07-a-05-s04 · Dev: That may be a useful characteristic, but a photograph would help the specialist more.
- p07-a-05-s05 · Mae: Should we classify it as a beetle for now?
- p07-a-05-s06 · Dev: Not until we're sure. Put it under unidentified insects and attach the photo.
- p07-a-05-s07 · Mae: All right. I'll photograph it where it is and record the location.

目标用法：

- bug · n. · 小昆虫的非正式称呼；义项 sense-staged-ox-f2462627a536234e-p07-a-05；来源 ox-f2462627a536234e；证据 p07-a-05-s01；词形 bug。
- bush · n. · 灌木；义项 sense-staged-ox-e1d5be2a5b313844-p07-a-05；来源 ox-e1d5be2a5b313844；证据 p07-a-05-s01；词形 bush。
- biological · adj. · 与生物有关的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7a96e975935a5a33-p07-a-05；来源 ox-7a96e975935a5a33；证据 p07-a-05-s02；词形 biological。
- closely · adv. · 仔细地；义项 sense-staged-ox-462aa436e50db258-p07-a-05；来源 ox-462aa436e50db258；证据 p07-a-05-s03；词形 closely。
- characteristic · n. · 可用来辨认的特征；义项 sense-staged-ox-60fd130b48a70c29-p07-a-05；来源 ox-60fd130b48a70c29；证据 p07-a-05-s04；词形 characteristic。
- classify · v. · 归入类别；义项 sense-staged-ox-2972545e49f5c4e0-p07-a-05；来源 ox-2972545e49f5c4e0；证据 p07-a-05-s05；词形 classify。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The volunteers distinguish visible observations from an uncertain identification, reject premature beetle classification, and choose a photograph plus location under unidentified insects. The title describes that preparation for expert identification, not an identification already made. All six words serve the same survey problem naturally.

- ox-f2462627a536234e：accepted；In s01, a tiny bug is an informal count-noun label for the insect noticed on the shrub; the later refusal to identify a beetle confirms that this is intentionally nontechnical, not a software fault.（p07-a-05-s01）。
- ox-e1d5be2a5b313844：accepted；In s01, that bush denotes the shrub carrying the insect. The demonstrative noun phrase locates a real survey observation and fits 灌木, not an idiom about wilderness.（p07-a-05-s01）。
- ox-7a96e975935a5a33：accepted；In s02, biological modifies survey to describe its focus on living organisms. The insect record and specialist identification make 与生物有关的 a substantive adjective sense, not merely decorative terminology.（p07-a-05-s02）。
- ox-462aa436e50db258：accepted；In s03, closely modifies look and means with careful attention, as shown by noticing two small spots. It does not mean physical proximity between two things.（p07-a-05-s03）。
- ox-60fd130b48a70c29：accepted；In s04, a useful characteristic refers to the two pale back spots observed in s03. The singular noun with an article denotes a potentially identifying feature without falsely claiming it uniquely identifies a species.（p07-a-05-s04）。
- ox-2972545e49f5c4e0：accepted；In s05, classify it as a beetle uses the transitive verb with an as-complement to propose category membership. The next turn explicitly withholds that classification pending certainty, preserving the selected categorization meaning.（p07-a-05-s05）。

## 分工准备蘑菇意面和生菜沙拉 · p07-a-06

主题：饮食沟通；情景：分工准备晚饭（shared-dinner-preparation）。

沟通目标：商定晚餐分工、菜品和份量

背景：室友使用现有食材准备两人的晚餐，并商量桌面布置。

人物：Ben、Iris。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-06-s01 · Ben: We've got mushrooms and a bunch of parsley. Shall we make pasta for the two of us?
- p07-a-06-s02 · Iris: Yes. Mushrooms and parsley are a good combination. I'll start the sauce if you chop the herbs.
- p07-a-06-s03 · Ben: What about these salad leaves? I tried one, and it's quite bitter.
- p07-a-06-s04 · Iris: Let's use a few with the mild lettuce, rather than making the whole salad from them.
- p07-a-06-s05 · Ben: Good idea. I'll make enough salad for two and put the rest of the lettuce away.
- p07-a-06-s06 · Iris: I'll set the table while the sauce cooks. Can we use that little candle in its glass holder?
- p07-a-06-s07 · Ben: Of course. I'll put it in the middle once I've finished chopping.

目标用法：

- bunch · n. · 一束或一把聚在一起的食材；义项 sense-staged-ox-452ca81191236e1e-p07-a-06；来源 ox-452ca81191236e1e；证据 p07-a-06-s01；词形 bunch。
- combination · n. · 搭配组合；义项 sense-staged-ox-0ecc96bd241d8108-p07-a-06；来源 ox-0ecc96bd241d8108；证据 p07-a-06-s02；词形 combination。
- chop · v. · 切碎；义项 sense-staged-ox-146b2421592e317a-p07-a-06；来源 ox-146b2421592e317a；证据 p07-a-06-s02；词形 chop。
- bitter · adj. · 味苦的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d73704bc54dc1765-p07-a-06；来源 ox-d73704bc54dc1765；证据 p07-a-06-s03；词形 bitter。
- candle · n. · 蜡烛；义项 sense-staged-ox-10f47fc8f3529f11-p07-a-06；来源 ox-10f47fc8f3529f11；证据 p07-a-06-s06；词形 candle。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The roommates settle on mushroom-and-parsley pasta, divide sauce and chopping tasks, limit bitter leaves in a two-person salad, and agree on a table candle. The candle arises through table setting rather than a disconnected vocabulary insertion. The reused dinner-preparation goal is met by agreed dishes, portions and responsibilities, not a claimed completed meal.

## 为旧漫画竞拍确定上限 · p07-a-07

主题：消费与服务；情景：确定旧漫画竞拍预算（vintage-comic-auction-limit）。

沟通目标：检查漫画品相及拍卖费用，确定包含费用的出价上限而不声称竞拍成功

背景：两位朋友在虚构的旧书拍卖预展上讨论一本漫画的状况和出价上限，没有进行实际竞拍。

人物：Jules、Nico。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-07-s01 · Jules: As a collector, would you pay much for this comic?
- p07-a-07-s02 · Nico: It's a classic superhero story, but this copy is missing its back cover.
- p07-a-07-s03 · Jules: The opening price is ten dollars. I was thinking of trying for it.
- p07-a-07-s04 · Nico: What can you afford, including the auction fee?
- p07-a-07-s05 · Jules: Twenty dollars altogether. I wouldn't spend another buck beyond that.
- p07-a-07-s06 · Nico: Then check the fee before you bid. Your limit needs to cover both.
- p07-a-07-s07 · Jules: The notice says there's no buyer's fee. I'll stop at twenty, even if someone else gets it.

- comic · n. · 连环漫画书；义项 sense-staged-ox-0293f63739161051-p07-a-07；来源 ox-0293f63739161051；证据 p07-a-07-s01；词形 comic。

- p07-a-08-s01 · Rosa: I'm blind. Does this exhibition have an audio description or a guided visit?

- p07-a-08-s04 · Kit: Certainly. We have portable chairs, and you can move one between the rooms.
- p07-a-08-s05 · Rosa: That would make a real difference to my comfort. Is there space on today's tour?
- p07-a-08-s06 · Kit: Yes. Would you like me to reserve a place and a chair?
- p07-a-08-s07 · Rosa: Please do. My name is Rosa Lane.

The receptionist's response follows the combined place-and-chair request. The title and reservation goal fit that ending, and discussion of colour is appropriate within an audio-described painting tour.

## 把咖啡馆调查结论限定在实际样本内 · p07-a-09

主题：研究与论证；情景：讨论调查证据（survey-evidence）。

沟通目标：核对样本、局限与结论的依据

背景：两名学生审查自己在一家咖啡馆收集的顾客问卷，避免把小样本解释成全城趋势。

人物：Leila、Finn。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-09-s01 · Leila: Our draft says the whole city prefers reusable cups. Is the evidence really that convincing?
- p07-a-09-s02 · Finn: No. We only asked people in one café, so there's a clear risk of bias.
- p07-a-09-s03 · Leila: But two other class groups got similar results. Isn't that enough?
- p07-a-09-s04 · Finn: It might be a coincidence. They used the same café on different days.
- p07-a-09-s05 · Leila: Then we can't claim any certainty about the city's habits.
- p07-a-09-s06 · Finn: Exactly. A comparative study across different neighbourhoods would tell us more.
- p07-a-09-s07 · Leila: Should we call our report a comprehensive survey?
- p07-a-09-s08 · Finn: Definitely not. Describe this sample and cite the three sets of questionnaires separately.
- p07-a-09-s09 · Leila: I'm convinced. I'll change the claim to 'Most people in our café sample preferred reusable cups.'
- p07-a-09-s10 · Finn: Good. I'll add the sample size and explain how the location limits the conclusion.

目标用法：

- convincing · adj. · 有说服力的；义项 sense-staged-ox-9b403c8d4b8845d5-p07-a-09；来源 ox-9b403c8d4b8845d5；证据 p07-a-09-s01；词形 convincing。
- bias · n. · 样本选择造成的偏差；义项 sense-staged-ox-47c41e7c83d35959-p07-a-09；来源 ox-47c41e7c83d35959；证据 p07-a-09-s02；词形 bias。
- coincidence · n. · 巧合；义项 sense-staged-ox-15fa408d725064af-p07-a-09；来源 ox-15fa408d725064af；证据 p07-a-09-s04；词形 coincidence。
- certainty · n. · 确信或确定性；义项 sense-staged-ox-72759642b39f8ccb-p07-a-09；来源 ox-72759642b39f8ccb；证据 p07-a-09-s05；词形 certainty。
- comparative · adj. · 比较性的；义项 sense-staged-ox-1ced392cc445ff55-p07-a-09；来源 ox-1ced392cc445ff55；证据 p07-a-09-s06；词形 comparative。
- comprehensive · adj. · 全面的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d659dc4a4d6861c7-p07-a-09；来源 ox-d659dc4a4d6861c7；证据 p07-a-09-s07；词形 comprehensive。
- cite · v. · 引用并注明资料来源；义项 sense-staged-ox-cbc47d6e1eb6bfc2-p07-a-09；来源 ox-cbc47d6e1eb6bfc2；证据 p07-a-09-s08；词形 cite。
- convinced · adj. · 确信的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7b9cdadf3dcba98b-p07-a-09；来源 ox-7b9cdadf3dcba98b；证据 p07-a-09-s09；词形 convinced。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The students challenge a citywide claim because all three groups sampled one café, reject an inflated comprehensive label, and agree to state the sample-specific result and its limitations. Suggesting coincidence does not establish it as fact; the repeated location remains the substantive sampling objection. The reused survey-evidence metadata in the base taxonomy fits the narrowed conclusion and agreed edits.

- ox-9b403c8d4b8845d5：accepted；In s01, convincing is a predicative adjective grading the evidence's persuasive force. The question challenges whether that evidence warrants the citywide claim, matching 有说服力的.（p07-a-09-s01）。
- ox-47c41e7c83d35959：accepted；In s02, risk of bias names possible systematic distortion caused by selecting respondents from only one café. The noun is a sampling problem, not personal hostility, and the Chinese sense identifies that distinction.（p07-a-09-s02）。
- ox-15fa408d725064af：accepted；In s04, a coincidence is a tentative explanation for matching results from separate class groups. Might keeps chance as a possibility rather than a proven cause; the count noun means 巧合 and the next sentence still notes the shared sampling limitation.（p07-a-09-s04）。
- ox-72759642b39f8ccb：accepted；In s05, any certainty about the city's habits is a noun phrase denoting justified confidence or certainty. Can't claim explicitly withholds it because the samples do not cover the city.（p07-a-09-s05）。
- ox-1ced392cc445ff55：accepted；In s06, comparative modifies study and proposes comparing different neighbourhoods. This directly remedies the single-location evidence problem and supports 比较性的.（p07-a-09-s06）。
- ox-d659dc4a4d6861c7：accepted；In s07, comprehensive modifies survey in a proposed report description. The reply rejects that label, naturally teaching the adjective's broad-coverage meaning without falsely claiming the actual study is comprehensive.（p07-a-09-s07）。
- ox-cbc47d6e1eb6bfc2：accepted；In s08, cite is an imperative transitive verb taking the three questionnaire sets as evidence sources. Separately motivates distinguishing the sources in the report and supports 引用并注明资料来源.（p07-a-09-s08）。
- ox-7b9cdadf3dcba98b：accepted；In s09, I'm convinced states Leila's resulting settled view after Finn's reasons. The following commitment to narrow the claim confirms the predicative adjective sense, distinct from the evidence being convincing.（p07-a-09-s09）。

## 因乳房肿块预约诊所评估 · p07-a-10

主题：健康与照护；情景：描述问题并预约就诊（routine-clinic-booking）。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-10-s01 · Alina: I'd like an appointment at the clinic. I've noticed a lump in my left breast.
- p07-a-10-s02 · Jo: When did you first notice it? I'll put that in the message for the clinician.
- p07-a-10-s03 · Alina: Yesterday. I'm concerned because my mother had cancer, and I don't know what this means.
- p07-a-10-s04 · Jo: I can arrange an assessment, but I can't tell you the cause over the phone. Would tomorrow at ten work for you?
- p07-a-10-s05 · Alina: Yes. Will the clinician have the message?
- p07-a-10-s06 · Jo: Yes, I've recorded what you told me. Your appointment is confirmed for ten tomorrow.
- p07-a-10-s07 · Alina: Thank you. I'll be there.

The patient reports the location, timing and reason for concern, while the receptionist records the information and confirms a next-day assessment without diagnosing the lump. The title accurately states an appointment for assessment, and the reused clinic-booking goal is met by an explicit ten o'clock confirmation. Register is calm and appropriate.

## 摔倒后请同伴联系现场急救员 · p07-a-11

主题：健康与照护；情景：摔倒后寻求帮助（walking-fall-assistance）。

- p07-a-11-s02 · Mina: Did you lose consciousness at any point?
- p07-a-11-s03 · Evan: No, I stayed conscious, but I felt as if I might collapse when I tried to stand.
- p07-a-11-s04 · Mina: I'll get the first-aid team by the entrance. Do you want me to stay with you while I call them?
- p07-a-11-s05 · Evan: Yes, please. I'm cold too. Could you put my blanket over my legs?
- p07-a-11-s06 · Mina: Here it is. I'm calling the team now and telling them exactly where we are.
- p07-a-11-s07 · Evan: Thanks. I'll wait here with you.

The fallen walker reports facial impact and weakness, answers the consciousness question, requests company and a blanket, and the companion starts contacting onsite first aid with their location. The ending explicitly remains waiting, consistent with the request-for-help goal rather than a claim of treatment or recovery. The interaction is coherent and not presented as a medical assessment.

## 排练战争短剧的撤离提示 · p07-a-13

主题：文娱与运动；情景：核对历史短剧撤离场景的提示（historical-play-scene-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：澄清虚构战争场景、录音提示与道具处理，约定慢速排练撤离段落

背景：业余剧团排练一出虚构的内战题材短剧，用录音表现轰炸，不使用武器或实弹。

人物：Dora、Felix。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-13-s01 · Dora: Before we start, does the whole cast understand where this scene takes place?
- p07-a-13-s02 · Felix: In a school during a civil war. My character is the commander trying to get everyone out.
- p07-a-13-s03 · Dora: Right. The bombing is represented by the recording, not anything happening on stage.
- p07-a-13-s04 · Felix: When do I give the command to leave? Before the noise or after it?
- p07-a-13-s05 · Dora: After the first sound. Everyone needs to hear your line clearly.
- p07-a-13-s06 · Felix: And what do I do with the object I find on the floor?
- p07-a-13-s07 · Dora: It's a wooden prop shaped like a bullet, not a real one. Leave it where it is; the audience only needs to notice it.

- p07-a-16-s05 · Noor: Maybe. I feel capable of explaining it in a conversation, just not to a room full of people.
- p07-a-16-s06 · Jasper: Then start as if you're explaining it to me. We only need to practise the opening today.

The exact ending agrees to add labels and the compound explanation, satisfying the reused revision goal without claiming the diagram has already been edited.

- p07-a-19-s01 · Gail: A loose brick has fallen from the garden wall. I've kept people away from that side.
- p07-a-19-s02 · Arun: Thanks. Do we know whether the rest of the wall is sound?
- p07-a-19-s03 · Gail: No. Someone suggested replacing it with concrete, but we haven't had it inspected.
- p07-a-19-s04 · Arun: Then we shouldn't choose the construction method yet. Let's consult a building professional first.
- p07-a-19-s05 · Gail: I can contact the consultant who inspected the hall last year.
- p07-a-19-s06 · Arun: Ask whether the wall can be repaired and what we'd need to know before we construct anything new.
- p07-a-19-s07 · Gail: I'll request an inspection and keep the area closed. We can compare the options after we have the report.
- p07-a-19-s08 · Arun: Agreed. Don't ask anyone to start work before that.

目标用法：

- brick · n. · 砖块；义项 sense-staged-ox-410909ffee879b65-p07-a-19；来源 ox-410909ffee879b65；证据 p07-a-19-s01；词形 brick。
- concrete · n. · 混凝土；义项 sense-staged-ox-58798b3ca48cb8dd-p07-a-19；来源 ox-58798b3ca48cb8dd；证据 p07-a-19-s03；词形 concrete。
- construction · n. · 建造；义项 sense-staged-ox-bd1fb53d7cd2f5f7-p07-a-19；来源 ox-bd1fb53d7cd2f5f7；证据 p07-a-19-s04；词形 construction。
- consult · v. · 向专业人士咨询；义项 sense-staged-ox-b154610382154f01-p07-a-19；来源 ox-b154610382154f01；证据 p07-a-19-s04；词形 consult。
- consultant · n. · 提供专业意见的顾问；义项 sense-staged-ox-4ca15d22f19a5331-p07-a-19；来源 ox-4ca15d22f19a5331；证据 p07-a-19-s05；词形 consultant。
- construct · v. · 建造；义项 sense-staged-ox-65e9ae562b5ddc6f-p07-a-19；来源 ox-65e9ae562b5ddc6f；证据 p07-a-19-s06；词形 construct。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

A fallen brick prompts concern about the wall, not a premature engineering prescription. The speakers defer the suggested concrete replacement until a professional inspection, assign contact with a known consultant and keep the area closed. The title and goal precisely match that inspection-first decision; the building vocabulary contributes to alternatives being deferred, not an invented finished repair.

- ox-410909ffee879b65：accepted；In s01, a loose brick is one physical masonry unit that has fallen from the garden wall. The count-noun article and loose modifier support 砖块.（p07-a-19-s01）。
- ox-58798b3ca48cb8dd：accepted；In s03, replacing it with concrete uses concrete as a mass noun for a possible replacement material. It is not the adjective meaning specific, and the suggestion is explicitly not yet adopted.（p07-a-19-s03）。
- ox-bd1fb53d7cd2f5f7：accepted；In s04, construction method is a noun-noun phrase describing how a new wall might be built. Deferring that method until inspection fits the process sense 建造, not a grammatical construction.（p07-a-19-s04）。
- ox-b154610382154f01：accepted；In s04, consult a building professional is a transitive verb construction for seeking that person's expert advice. It does not incorrectly require with, and the following contact plan supplies a concrete consultation step.（p07-a-19-s04）。
- ox-4ca15d22f19a5331：accepted；In s05, the consultant who inspected the hall is a specific previously used professional adviser. The relative clause makes the occupational noun meaningful in the inspection plan.（p07-a-19-s05）。
- ox-65e9ae562b5ddc6f：accepted；In s06, construct anything new refers to physically building a replacement or new structure only after obtaining information. The transitive verb and before-clause fit 建造 without implying authorization to begin.（p07-a-19-s06）。

## 选用较小场地并保留预算余量 · p07-a-20

主题：工作与协作；情景：调整活动预算（event-budget）。

沟通目标：比较场地方案并明确预算取舍

背景：活动筹备者比较两个虚构场地的报价，为四十人的工作坊选择场地。

人物：Imani、Seth。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-20-s01 · Imani: The larger hall has a capacity of a hundred, but we're expecting forty people.
- p07-a-20-s02 · Seth: The smaller room holds fifty and costs considerably less: three hundred pounds rather than six hundred.
- p07-a-20-s03 · Imani: Let's calculate the whole cost. Are chairs and cleaning included in both prices?
- p07-a-20-s04 · Seth: Yes. I've checked the contract for each room. Both totals include them.
- p07-a-20-s05 · Imani: A three-hundred-pound saving is considerable. Still, we should make a conservative estimate of what we'll collect in ticket sales.
- p07-a-20-s06 · Seth: Agreed. If we budget for thirty paying guests, we won't depend on a full house.
- p07-a-20-s07 · Imani: That makes cost our main consideration, provided everyone can fit comfortably.
- p07-a-20-s08 · Seth: The smaller room's seated layout has space for fifty, so forty will fit.
- p07-a-20-s09 · Imani: Then let's choose that room and keep the saving as a reserve. I'll ask them for the booking form.

目标用法：

- capacity · n. · 场地的容纳人数；义项 sense-staged-ox-f61b8a289b3e88b8-p07-a-20；来源 ox-f61b8a289b3e88b8；证据 p07-a-20-s01；词形 capacity。
- considerably · adv. · 相当大程度地；义项 sense-staged-ox-7a172593a465250b-p07-a-20；来源 ox-7a172593a465250b；证据 p07-a-20-s02；词形 considerably。
- calculate · v. · 计算；义项 sense-staged-ox-566365213ead0b88-p07-a-20；来源 ox-566365213ead0b88；证据 p07-a-20-s03；词形 calculate。
- contract · n. · 合同；义项 sense-staged-ox-cb31eaafed428149-p07-a-20；来源 ox-cb31eaafed428149；证据 p07-a-20-s04；词形 contract。
- considerable · adj. · 相当大的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8d3007ec3928824a-p07-a-20；来源 ox-8d3007ec3928824a；证据 p07-a-20-s05；词形 considerable。
- conservative · adj. · 保守谨慎的；义项 sense-staged-ox-597f693f6fbfbdff-p07-a-20；来源 ox-597f693f6fbfbdff；证据 p07-a-20-s05；词形 conservative。
- consideration · n. · 作决定时要考虑的因素；义项 sense-staged-ox-1179bd3c96471b78-p07-a-20；来源 ox-1179bd3c96471b78；证据 p07-a-20-s07；词形 consideration。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The organisers compare 100- and 50-person rooms against forty attendees, verify included costs, acknowledge the £300 saving and adopt cautious revenue assumptions. The closing choice of the smaller room and request for a form matches the reused event-budget goal in the base taxonomy. The title concerns selecting and reserving budget headroom, not a completed venue booking.

- ox-f61b8a289b3e88b8：accepted；In s01, a capacity of a hundred quantifies the hall's maximum people rather than ability to do a task. The comparison with forty attendees makes the selected venue-occupancy noun sense exact.（p07-a-20-s01）。
- ox-7a172593a465250b：accepted；In s02, considerably modifies less in costs considerably less. The £300 versus £600 comparison provides a concrete substantial degree of difference for the adverb.（p07-a-20-s02）。
- ox-566365213ead0b88：accepted；In s03, calculate the whole cost is a transitive verb proposing a complete cost calculation. The follow-up question about chairs and cleaning ensures the quoted prices are comparable rather than introducing an unrelated arithmetic exercise.（p07-a-20-s03）。
- ox-cb31eaafed428149：accepted；In s04, the contract for each room names the written hire terms that specify included items. Checking these documents before requesting a booking form is coherent; the noun does not imply either contract has already been signed.（p07-a-20-s04）。
- ox-8d3007ec3928824a：accepted；In s05, considerable is a predicative adjective evaluating the three-hundred-pound saving as substantial. This complements, rather than misuses, the earlier adverb considerably in the price comparison.（p07-a-20-s05）。
- ox-597f693f6fbfbdff：accepted；In s05, a conservative estimate describes cautious expected ticket revenue. The next turn budgets for thirty paying guests rather than relying on all forty expected attendees, distinguishing this sense from political conservatism.（p07-a-20-s05）。
- ox-1179bd3c96471b78：accepted；In s07, our main consideration identifies cost as the deciding factor, explicitly conditional on comfortable capacity. The count noun is an issue to weigh, not courteous treatment of another person.（p07-a-20-s07）。

## 缩小社区资助草稿的范围并分工补充细节 · p07-a-21

主题：工作与协作；情景：缩小社区活动资助草稿范围（community-grant-draft-review）。

沟通目标：将宽泛活动提案聚焦为邻里园艺交流，分工补充实际资源并安排委员会提交前核对

背景：社区组织的主席和志愿者审查尚未提交的活动资助草稿。

人物：Ruth、Jonah。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-21-s01 · Ruth: As chairman of the committee, I'd like us to agree on a clearer plan before we submit this.
- p07-a-21-s02 · Jonah: Is the current proposal too broad? It covers everything from gardening to evening classes.
- p07-a-21-s03 · Ruth: Yes. What's the core purpose of the project?
- p07-a-21-s04 · Jonah: Helping new residents meet their neighbours. We could start with two shared gardening sessions.
- p07-a-21-s05 · Ruth: Good. The first session could comprise a short introduction, paired planting, and tea afterwards.
- p07-a-21-s06 · Jonah: What can the volunteers contribute besides their time?
- p07-a-21-s07 · Ruth: Some have offered spare tools. We should describe that contribution without putting an invented price on it.
- p07-a-21-s08 · Jonah: I'll narrow the activities and list the tools that have actually been offered.
- p07-a-21-s09 · Ruth: And I'll ask the other members to check the plan before we send anything.

- chairman · n. · 委员会主席；义项 sense-staged-ox-64ad57b3023b7caa-p07-a-21；来源 ox-64ad57b3023b7caa；证据 p07-a-21-s01；词形 chairman。
- committee · n. · 委员会；义项 sense-staged-ox-b570be12ceb6eabc-p07-a-21；来源 ox-b570be12ceb6eabc；证据 p07-a-21-s01；词形 committee。
- broad · adj. · 范围宽泛的；义项 sense-staged-ox-5fd9e469456d69e7-p07-a-21；来源 ox-5fd9e469456d69e7；证据 p07-a-21-s02；词形 broad。
- core · adj. · 核心的；义项 sense-staged-ox-03d91da83502f99b-p07-a-21；来源 ox-03d91da83502f99b；证据 p07-a-21-s03；词形 core。

- contribute · v. · 贡献或提供；义项 sense-staged-ox-6b1c8ba8232439e3-p07-a-21；来源 ox-6b1c8ba8232439e3；证据 p07-a-21-s06；词形 contribute。
- contribution · n. · 贡献的物品或帮助；义项 sense-staged-ox-0cb7dbf20a1fd721-p07-a-21；来源 ox-0cb7dbf20a1fd721；证据 p07-a-21-s07；词形 contribution。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The committee chair and volunteer narrow an overbroad grant proposal to two social gardening sessions, outline one session and identify actual donated tools without inventing a value. They divide the revision and committee-check tasks before submission. The formal vocabulary is appropriate to proposal review, and the title and goal faithfully describe agreed changes rather than a submitted grant.

- ox-64ad57b3023b7caa：accepted；In s01, As chairman of the committee states Ruth's official leadership role. Chairman is a traditional title that can be used by a woman; nothing in the dialogue makes its selected committee-chair noun sense inconsistent.（p07-a-21-s01）。
- ox-b570be12ceb6eabc：accepted；In s01, the committee is the decision-making group whose chair is speaking. The final reference to the other members checking the plan sustains the group-noun meaning.（p07-a-21-s01）。
- ox-5fd9e469456d69e7：accepted；In s02, too broad describes the proposal's overly wide scope, immediately illustrated by unrelated activities from gardening to classes. It does not describe physical width.（p07-a-21-s02）。
- ox-03d91da83502f99b：accepted；In s03, core modifies purpose to ask for the project's central objective. The answer about helping neighbours meet specifies what is central, supporting the selected adjective record.（p07-a-21-s03）。

This is the correct whole-to-parts direction of the transitive verb and fits formal planning speech.（p07-a-21-s05）。
- ox-6b1c8ba8232439e3：accepted；In s06, What can the volunteers contribute asks what they can provide beyond their time. The next turn's offered tools supplies the transitive verb's intended material contribution.（p07-a-21-s06）。

## 弄清课程结业证书的取得条件 · p07-a-22

主题：学习与技能；情景：询问课程结业证书要求（course-certificate-requirements）。

沟通目标：澄清出勤、项目与考试要求，并索取书面说明以便在报名前核对时间

背景：学员在决定是否报名之前，向课程工作人员核对结业要求。

人物：Cleo、Mason。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-22-s01 · Cleo: The course page mentions a certificate. Do you receive it just for attending?
- p07-a-22-s02 · Mason: No. We issue it on completion of the final project, provided you've also attended four of the five sessions.
- p07-a-22-s03 · Cleo: Is the last session compulsory? I may be away that evening.
- p07-a-22-s04 · Mason: Attendance at that session isn't compulsory, but the project still has to be submitted by Friday.
- p07-a-22-s05 · Cleo: That sounds less complicated than I thought. Is there an exam as well?
- p07-a-22-s06 · Mason: No exam. The project is the only assessed piece of work. I can send you the written requirements.
- p07-a-22-s07 · Cleo: Please do. I'll check the dates against my travel plans before I sign up.

目标用法：

- certificate · n. · 结业证书；义项 sense-staged-ox-ca4f7f0e03ecd968-p07-a-22；来源 ox-ca4f7f0e03ecd968；证据 p07-a-22-s01；词形 certificate。
- completion · n. · 完成；义项 sense-staged-ox-a825f27952890b3a-p07-a-22；来源 ox-a825f27952890b3a；证据 p07-a-22-s02；词形 completion。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The prospective learner distinguishes attendance from completing the project, clarifies that the last session is optional within a four-of-five rule, and learns there is no exam. Asking for written requirements and postponing signup until dates are checked matches the goal exactly. The staff member does not falsely promise the certificate merely for being present.

## 约定比较两种花店标志草图 · p07-a-23

- p07-a-23-s01 · Talia: The current logo feels rather formal. Could it be more colorful?

- p07-a-23-s07 · Talia: I like that direction. Could I see it in yellow and green, and another version in just green?
- p07-a-23-s08 · Wes: I'll make those two sketches for you to compare before we choose the final colors.

## 为失约道歉并重新约好见面 · p07-a-24

主题：家庭与人际；情景：向朋友道歉并重新安排见面（friend-apology-and-reconnection）。

沟通目标：承认失约及其影响，澄清彼此感受并约定下一次见面

背景：两位大学时期的好友谈论上周未能成行的见面，试着恢复联系。

人物：Ari、Bea。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-24-s01 · Ari: I'm sorry I left you waiting last Saturday. I need to confess something: I didn't miss a train. I forgot our plan.
- p07-a-24-s02 · Bea: I was hurt, especially when you blamed the transport. Why didn't you just tell me?
- p07-a-24-s03 · Ari: I was embarrassed. There's no one else to blame, and I should have been honest.
- p07-a-24-s04 · Bea: We had such a strong bond at university. I miss seeing you, but I need to know when you're actually coming.
- p07-a-24-s05 · Ari: I miss it too. Could we try again this Sunday at eleven? I'll put it in my calendar now.
- p07-a-24-s06 · Bea: Yes, at the café near my flat. Besides, we never finished talking about your new job.
- p07-a-24-s07 · Ari: Eleven on Sunday, then. If anything changes, I'll tell you before you leave home.
- p07-a-24-s08 · Bea: All right. I'm glad you told me the truth.

目标用法：

- confess · v. · 承认自己做错或隐瞒的事；义项 sense-staged-ox-26dd4f4592e7d65a-p07-a-24；来源 ox-26dd4f4592e7d65a；证据 p07-a-24-s01；词形 confess。

Ari admits forgetting the meeting and lying about transport, Bea names the hurt without immediate dismissal, and they agree on Sunday at eleven at the café with advance notice of changes. The title and relationship goal are met by an explicit apology and renewed plan, not an unsupported claim that all trust is restored.

## 为展览争议报道选取平衡的短片段 · p07-a-25

主题：工作与协作；情景：修改展览收费争议报道的片段选择（exhibition-news-clip-edit）。

- p07-a-25-s04 · Omar: My chief concern is that our current edit includes three complaints but no explanation from the gallery.

- p07-a-25-s06 · Omar: Then use a clip of that answer alongside one resident's objection. Don't make either speaker sound as if they represent everyone.
- p07-a-25-s07 · Keira: I'll revise the edit and keep the price and opening times on screen.
- p07-a-25-s08 · Omar: Send me that version before it goes to the station. I'll check the quotes against the full interviews.

The journalist and editor clarify the short report's dual art-and-fee focus, identify the imbalance of complaints without the gallery's answer, and agree to add the manager's explanation beside a resident objection. Keeping factual details onscreen and checking quotes before delivery matches the goal. ## 区分课堂虚构案例中的大会和立法机构 · p07-a-26

主题：学习与技能；情景：核对虚构公民教育案例中的机构关系（civics-congress-presentation-check）。

- p07-a-26-s01 · Luca: In our fictional republic, is the party convention the same thing as the congress?
- p07-a-26-s02 · Esme: No. Look at the case notes: the party meets at a convention to choose its candidate. The congress is the national law-making body.
- p07-a-26-s03 · Luca: So every citizen who votes in an election is also a member of the congress?
- p07-a-26-s04 · Esme: Not in this case. Voters choose representatives; the representatives hold the seats.
- p07-a-26-s05 · Luca: I see. My diagram mixes the voters, the party meeting, and the elected body together.
- p07-a-26-s06 · Esme: Give them separate boxes and label what each does.
- p07-a-26-s07 · Luca: I'll redraw it that way and use the case notes rather than guessing from the names.

- congress · n. · 国家立法机构；义项 sense-staged-ox-c34754561daee6c9-p07-a-26；来源 ox-c34754561daee6c9；证据 p07-a-26-s01；词形 congress。
- citizen · n. · 公民；义项 sense-staged-ox-f48a0668dc34b4e8-p07-a-26；来源 ox-f48a0668dc34b4e8；证据 p07-a-26-s03；词形 citizen。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The students distinguish a fictional party nomination meeting from the legislature, then correct the further confusion between voters and elected representatives. The decision to redraw three labelled boxes using the case notes achieves the diagram-revision plan exactly. The invented case is clearly marked and does not make unsupported claims about a real country's constitutional rules.

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-27-s01 · Yara: This label says the bowl shows what everyone in the civilization ate. Isn't that too much to claim from one object?

- p07-a-27-s03 · Yara: Does it tell us what was served in them?
- p07-a-27-s04 · Hugh: Not for this bowl. The contents haven't been identified.
- p07-a-27-s05 · Yara: Then I'll describe where this type was found and remove the claim about everyone's diet.

The volunteers notice that one bowl cannot justify a claim about everyone's diet, consult the limited catalogue evidence and agree to retain the find-location statement with a traceable number. The title and goal describe a properly bounded label revision rather than a proven ancient diet. Catalogue and civilization spellings are compatible with British usage that permits -ize forms.

## 用同一套条件重测传感器电路 · p07-a-28

主题：研究与论证；情景：核对传感器试验的可比性（sensor-test-report-revision）。

沟通目标：发现测试距离变化使数据不可比，约定固定位置重测后再修订可靠性结论

背景：两名学生核对低压课堂传感器实验的数据，发现测试条件不一致，决定按教师要求重测。

人物：Rin、Oscar。

起草：author-phase07-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-a-28-s01 · Rin: Our circuit worked every time, but the readings aren't consistent. Can we still say the sensor is reliable?
- p07-a-28-s02 · Oscar: Not yet. Did we put the object at the same distance each time?
- p07-a-28-s03 · Rin: No. We moved it closer in the last three tests.
- p07-a-28-s04 · Oscar: Consequently, those results aren't directly comparable with the first three.
- p07-a-28-s05 · Rin: Broadly speaking, it detects the object, but we haven't shown how accurately it measures distance.
- p07-a-28-s06 · Oscar: Right. Let's follow the teacher's test sheet and keep the distance fixed. Then we can see whether it responds consistently.
- p07-a-28-s07 · Rin: I'll mark the position on the desk and repeat the tests before we revise the report.

目标用法：

- circuit · n. · 电路；义项 sense-staged-ox-c5d6877197b1e4b5-p07-a-28；来源 ox-c5d6877197b1e4b5；证据 p07-a-28-s01；词形 circuit。
- consistent · adj. · 前后一致的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b968ae77690bdb98-p07-a-28；来源 ox-b968ae77690bdb98；证据 p07-a-28-s01；词形 consistent。
- consequently · adv. · 因此；义项 sense-staged-ox-f0f62389ab032c60-p07-a-28；来源 ox-f0f62389ab032c60；证据 p07-a-28-s04；词形 consequently。
- broadly · adv. · 大体上；义项 sense-staged-ox-fccaa89b3dab7b30-p07-a-28；来源 ox-fccaa89b3dab7b30；证据 p07-a-28-s05；词形 broadly。
- consistently · adv. · 始终如一地；义项 sense-staged-ox-909122c1b403dfb6-p07-a-28；来源 ox-909122c1b403dfb6；证据 p07-a-28-s06；词形 consistently。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The students correctly separate a circuit operating from proof of measurement reliability. They discover that the object moved between trials, explain why raw readings cannot test repeatability on those conditions, and agree to mark a fixed position and repeat before revising the report. The goal and title match the retest plan, not a proven accuracy result. Slightly formal connectors suit a classroom report discussion.

- ox-c5d6877197b1e4b5：accepted；In s01, our circuit names the classroom sensor's electrical arrangement that functioned on each attempt. The sensor and readings context excludes a travel route or sporting circuit.（p07-a-28-s01）。
- ox-b968ae77690bdb98：accepted；In s01, the readings aren't consistent uses a predicative adjective to describe differing results. The subsequent diagnosis explains that distances changed, so this observation is not itself treated as proof of defective equipment.（p07-a-28-s01）。
- ox-f0f62389ab032c60：accepted；In s04, Consequently marks the inference from changed object distance to lack of directly comparable results. It is a sentence adverb expressing a consequence, with ordinary initial capitalization of the approved lowercase form.（p07-a-28-s04）。
- ox-fccaa89b3dab7b30：accepted；In s05, Broadly speaking introduces the limited overall finding that the sensor detects an object, then explicitly withholds a conclusion about distance accuracy. The idiomatic adverb phrase matches 大体上 and its scope is appropriately qualified.（p07-a-28-s05）。

- p07-a-29-s01 · Demi: Our electricity consumption went up last month. Do you think the new heater caused it?
- p07-a-29-s02 · Paul: It might have contributed, but we were both home more often too.
- p07-a-29-s03 · Demi: Then the bill alone won't tell us which change mattered most.
- p07-a-29-s04 · Paul: Let's record the meter reading at the same time each evening for a week, along with when the heater is on.
- p07-a-29-s05 · Demi: I'll make a shared note. We can look at the pattern before deciding what to change.
- p07-a-29-s06 · Paul: Agreed. I'll take the first reading tonight.

The roommates consider both a new heater and more time at home instead of assigning causation from the bill alone. They agree on same-time daily meter readings with heater-use notes for one week and assign the first reading. The title and goal accurately state an observation plan and later comparison, not immediate savings or a causal conclusion.

- p07-a-31-s05 · Nell: Can I send the image online, or do you need a print?

- p07-b-02-s07 · Ruth: Noon tomorrow. That's our editorial deadline, not the date the council will decide anything.
- p07-b-02-s08 · Amir: Understood. I'll compare the draft with the route proposal and clearly label it as a proposal.
- p07-b-02-s09 · Ruth: Good. Send me the revised text and the document link together.
- p07-b-02-s10 · Amir: I will. I'll call the transport office this afternoon about anything the document doesn't explain.

## 约定先恢复十分钟钢琴练习 · p07-b-04

主题：文娱与运动；情景：商定恢复钢琴练习的一小步（piano-practice-restart-plan）。

背景：成年钢琴学习者埃斯特尔向朋友乔纳谈论停练后的顾虑。

人物：Estelle、Jonah。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-04-s01 · Estelle: I haven't touched the piano since that awful performance. It takes courage even to open the music now.

- p07-b-04-s03 · Estelle: Maybe. I have the determination to improve, but I don't have the discipline to practise when I'm tired.
- p07-b-04-s04 · Jonah: Then don't start with an hour. Could you devote ten minutes to it after breakfast?
- p07-b-04-s05 · Estelle: I could. I just don't want to disappoint my teacher again.
- p07-b-04-s06 · Jonah: One difficult performance doesn't erase your progress. Don't let it discourage you from playing altogether.
- p07-b-04-s07 · Estelle: All right. I'll try ten minutes tomorrow, with no recording and no audience.
- p07-b-04-s08 · Jonah: That sounds reasonable. Would you like to tell me how it went afterwards?
- p07-b-04-s09 · Estelle: Yes, but please ask about the practice, not whether it was perfect.

## 明确西部短剧门响后的出场提示 · p07-b-05

- p07-b-05-s03 · Bea: The crew thought you'd missed your cue, so they played the door sound twice.
- p07-b-05-s04 · Oscar: Oh, that explains it. I want a dramatic pause, but not one that looks like a mistake.
- p07-b-05-s05 · Bea: What duration do you have in mind? Two seconds, or closer to five?
- p07-b-05-s06 · Oscar: Two should be enough. Then I'll take one step through the doorway.
- p07-b-05-s07 · Bea: Let's use the first door sound as the cue and keep the lights steady while you wait.
- p07-b-05-s08 · Oscar: Agreed. Can we start again from the door sound so everyone can see the timing?

## 发现二手桌裂缝后决定不买 · p07-b-06

主题：消费与服务；情景：查看二手桌状况后决定不购买（secondhand-table-condition-choice）。

沟通目标：核对裂缝、桌腿和价格后决定不购买有损坏的桌子

背景：顾客米娜正在二手家具店查看一张带雕刻桌腿的小桌子。

人物：Mina、Hugh。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-06-s01 · Mina: You're the dealer I spoke to about the small oak table, aren't you?
- p07-b-06-s02 · Hugh: Yes. This is it. It's a decent table for the price, though it isn't in perfect condition.
- p07-b-06-s03 · Mina: I like the curved legs. Is that dark line part of the decoration?
- p07-b-06-s04 · Hugh: No, that's a crack. It goes along the side of the leg.
- p07-b-06-s05 · Mina: Has anyone checked whether the leg is still strong enough?
- p07-b-06-s06 · Hugh: Not professionally. I can lower the price, but I can't promise the leg won't need repair.
- p07-b-06-s07 · Mina: Then I'll leave it. I need a table I can use immediately, not another repair project.
- p07-b-06-s08 · Hugh: Fair enough. I'll let you know if a similar one arrives without that damage.

目标用法：

- dealer · n. · 经营特定商品的商人；义项 sense-staged-ox-6e196b6a4b35f60b-p07-b-06；来源 ox-6e196b6a4b35f60b；证据 p07-b-06-s01；词形 dealer。
- decent · adj. · 尚好的；质量过得去的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0ef7582dc892aba2-p07-b-06；来源 ox-0ef7582dc892aba2；证据 p07-b-06-s02；词形 decent。
- curved · adj. · 弯曲的；有曲线的；义项 sense-staged-ox-a36ea0b50214d84f-p07-b-06；来源 ox-a36ea0b50214d84f；证据 p07-b-06-s03；词形 curved。
- decoration · n. · 装饰图案或装饰部分；义项 sense-staged-ox-f4a2fca5dcf2d4ed-p07-b-06；来源 ox-f4a2fca5dcf2d4ed；证据 p07-b-06-s03；词形 decoration。
- crack · n. · 裂缝；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c3ea952a5d70b35-p07-b-06；来源 ox-1c3ea952a5d70b35；证据 p07-b-06-s04；词形 crack。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The shop conversation progresses from visible design to a disclosed crack and an uncertain repair need, then an explicit refusal to buy. Discussion of lowering the price does not substitute for condition assurance. The title and no-purchase goal are fulfilled; the price aspect is a proposed reduction rather than a quoted figure.

## 决定保留手工礼盒上的朴素设计 · p07-b-07

主题：文娱与运动；情景：为手工作品确定最后的装饰（first-craft-gift-finish）。

沟通目标：讨论制作体验和送礼心意并决定保留现有设计

背景：手工课学员莉娜给姐姐制作了一个小礼盒，临下课时请老师帕维尔看成品。

人物：Lena、Pavel。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-07-s01 · Lena: This is my first attempt at a craft since school. I expected a much neater box.
- p07-b-07-s02 · Pavel: The lid fits, and your painted leaves work well. Are you unhappy with the colours?
- p07-b-07-s03 · Lena: No, but there's a lot of empty space. Maybe my creation needs more decoration.
- p07-b-07-s04 · Pavel: Creativity isn't only about adding things. Leaving some space can be a choice too.
- p07-b-07-s05 · Lena: My sister likes simple designs. She'd probably be delighted with it as it is.
- p07-b-07-s06 · Pavel: Then why not stop here? You've made something that suits her.
- p07-b-07-s07 · Lena: I will. Making a gift for her has been a real delight, even with all the glue on my fingers.
- p07-b-07-s08 · Pavel: Let it dry on the shelf, and you can take it home at the end of class.

目标用法：

- craft · n. · 手工艺；义项 sense-staged-ox-d454e7a08064fc8c-p07-b-07；来源 ox-d454e7a08064fc8c；证据 p07-b-07-s01；词形 craft。
- creation · n. · 创作出的作品；义项 sense-staged-ox-8529b04d3cda1caf-p07-b-07；来源 ox-8529b04d3cda1caf；证据 p07-b-07-s03；词形 creation。
- creativity · n. · 创造力；义项 sense-staged-ox-7c0eadca48bdf239-p07-b-07；来源 ox-7c0eadca48bdf239；证据 p07-b-07-s04；词形 creativity。
- delighted · adj. · 非常高兴的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4d91974c695553cf-p07-b-07；来源 ox-4d91974c695553cf；证据 p07-b-07-s05；词形 delighted。
- delight · n. · 令人愉快的事情或体验；义项 sense-staged-ox-e21e0d796273f2b8-p07-b-07；来源 ox-e21e0d796273f2b8；证据 p07-b-07-s07；词形 delight。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The learner’s impulse to fill empty space is reconsidered against the recipient’s taste for simple designs. She chooses to stop decorating, then waits for the gift to dry. The craft-class identities and Chinese keep-the-design outcome match the complete exchange; no delivery to the sister is falsely claimed.

- ox-8529b04d3cda1caf：accepted；In s03, my creation is the box she has made, not the abstract act of creating; it is the object potentially needing decoration.（p07-b-07-s03）。
- ox-7c0eadca48bdf239：accepted；In s04, Creativity is the abstract inventive capacity involved in design choices, including restraint, so the mass noun sense fits.（p07-b-07-s04）。
- ox-4d91974c695553cf：accepted；In s05, be delighted with it describes the sister’s anticipated strong pleasure in the simple gift; with supplies the natural complement.（p07-b-07-s05）。

## 数据库崩溃后先保留原件并检查备份 · p07-b-08

主题：工作与协作；情景：数据库崩溃后约定先检查备份（database-crash-backup-check）。

沟通目标：保留原文件并分工检查磁盘与备份再决定恢复操作

背景：办公室数据库导入失败后，两位同事商定排查顺序；对话不声称已经恢复数据。

人物：Tessa、Robin。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-08-s01 · Tessa: Did the database crash while you were importing the orders?
- p07-b-08-s02 · Robin: Yes. The program closed, and now it won't open the file.
- p07-b-08-s03 · Tessa: Did the disk check detect any errors?
- p07-b-08-s04 · Robin: I haven't run it yet. Should I delete the damaged file and start again?
- p07-b-08-s05 · Tessa: Not yet. We don't know whether the file is damaged, and yesterday's backup may be missing today's orders.
- p07-b-08-s06 · Robin: Then I'll leave the original alone and check which backup files we have.
- p07-b-08-s07 · Tessa: Good. I'll ask IT to check the disk before we try opening another copy.
- p07-b-08-s08 · Robin: Let's compare what we find before making any changes.

目标用法：

- database · n. · 数据库；义项 sense-staged-ox-eacf62619e2ca904-p07-b-08；来源 ox-eacf62619e2ca904；证据 p07-b-08-s01；词形 database。
- crash · v. · 计算机程序突然崩溃停止运行；义项 sense-staged-ox-632c7be5b4e35b1c-p07-b-08；来源 ox-632c7be5b4e35b1c；证据 p07-b-08-s01；词形 crash。
- disk · n. · 计算机磁盘；义项 sense-staged-ox-26658cea5bd21257-p07-b-08；来源 ox-26658cea5bd21257；证据 p07-b-08-s03；词形 disk。
- detect · v. · 检测出；发现；义项 sense-staged-ox-8520d466a0b8d15f-p07-b-08；来源 ox-8520d466a0b8d15f；证据 p07-b-08-s03；词形 detect。
- delete · v. · 删除数据或文件；义项 sense-staged-ox-4572dc14d0784b5c-p07-b-08；来源 ox-4572dc14d0784b5c；证据 p07-b-08-s04；词形 delete。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The colleagues distinguish an application failure from an unproved corrupt-file diagnosis, reject premature deletion, and allocate backup inventory and IT disk checks. The final agreement is to compare findings before changing anything. Chinese metadata explicitly describes this plan rather than completed recovery, and the technical terms are coherent.

- ox-632c7be5b4e35b1c：accepted；In s01, crash is a bare intransitive verb following did, explained by the program abruptly closing in s02 rather than a physical collision.（p07-b-08-s01）。
- ox-26658cea5bd21257：accepted；In s03, disk modifies check as a noun naming computer storage; the later request for IT inspection maintains that sense. Disk is appropriate computing spelling.（p07-b-08-s03）。

- p07-b-09-s01 · Asha: That little seal is cute. Can we see it swimming from here?
- p07-b-09-s02 · Kit: Sometimes, but the underwater window gives you a better view of its dive.
- p07-b-09-s03 · Asha: I'm curious about the depth of the pool. It looks shallow from this side.
- p07-b-09-s04 · Kit: The sign says four metres at the deep end. The floor slopes downwards beyond those rocks.
- p07-b-09-s05 · Asha: So the creature hasn't disappeared behind them. It's probably swimming below the window ledge.
- p07-b-09-s06 · Kit: Possibly. We can't see all of the pool from one spot.
- p07-b-09-s07 · Asha: Let's go to the lower window and wait quietly instead of tapping on the glass.

## 确定旱灾援助下一轮的物资分发安排 · p07-b-10

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排旱灾援助物资的下一轮分发（drought-relief-delivery-plan）。

沟通目标：核对农户求助与现有物资并确定分发次序及联系人

背景：虚构旱灾救助站的工作人员依据现场登记记录安排物资，不涉及真实灾情或政策。

人物：Samira、Owen。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-10-s01 · Samira: The drought has left several farms without enough water. The calls this morning sound desperate.
- p07-b-10-s02 · Owen: What do the farmers say they need most urgently?
- p07-b-10-s03 · Samira: Drinking water first. One family has lost its entire crop and desperately needs a delivery today.
- p07-b-10-s04 · Owen: The destruction of the crops is terrible, but we can't solve the whole crisis with one van.
- p07-b-10-s05 · Samira: Agreed. We have enough bottled water for today's registered households. How should we distribute it?
- p07-b-10-s06 · Owen: Let's start with the people who cannot reach the collection point. I'll phone them to confirm they're home.
- p07-b-10-s07 · Samira: I'll prepare the boxes and update the distribution list so nobody gets missed or counted twice.
- p07-b-10-s08 · Owen: Good. Once the driver confirms the route, we can give those households an arrival window.
- p07-b-10-s09 · Samira: I'll also keep the other requests on the list for tomorrow. We shouldn't promise supplies we don't yet have.

- drought · n. · 长期干旱；义项 sense-staged-ox-0e611a4bbfab535a-p07-b-10；来源 ox-0e611a4bbfab535a；证据 p07-b-10-s01；词形 drought。

- crop · n. · 一季的农作物收成；义项 sense-staged-ox-0f7c4d2c5fd23221-p07-b-10；来源 ox-0f7c4d2c5fd23221；证据 p07-b-10-s03；词形 crop。
- desperately · adv. · 极其迫切地；义项 sense-staged-ox-0ad857ff551374ca-p07-b-10；来源 ox-0ad857ff551374ca；证据 p07-b-10-s03；词形 desperately。
- destruction · n. · 毁坏；破坏；义项 sense-staged-ox-b13eef34075ea21f-p07-b-10；来源 ox-b13eef34075ea21f；证据 p07-b-10-s04；词形 destruction。

- distribute · v. · 分发；分配物资；义项 sense-staged-ox-4a6ed05523ed2e83-p07-b-10；来源 ox-4a6ed05523ed2e83；证据 p07-b-10-s05；词形 distribute。
- distribution · n. · 分发；配送；义项 sense-staged-ox-ed9d3556c9ec0bd6-p07-b-10；来源 ox-ed9d3556c9ec0bd6；证据 p07-b-10-s07；词形 distribution。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The relief workers identify potable-water demand, distinguish available stock from unresolved future requests, prioritise people unable to collect it, and divide calling and packing/list duties. The route and arrival windows remain conditional, so no delivery success is invented. The Chinese goal of prioritisation and contact arrangements matches the achieved plan.

## 选定一部适合讨论的短篇小说集 · p07-b-11

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择读书小组作品（reading-group-book-choice）。

沟通目标：比较故事题材与讨论安排后选定共读作品

人物：Nell、Victor。

- p07-b-11-s01 · Nell: A critic on the radio recommended The Glass Road. Shall we read that next?
- p07-b-11-s02 · Victor: I tried it. The opening was so dull that I kept putting it down.
- p07-b-11-s03 · Nell: Really? It's been critically acclaimed. What didn't you like?
- p07-b-11-s04 · Victor: Nothing seemed to happen for fifty pages. It's about a detective chasing an art thief, but I'd rather read The Last Orchard, the short-story collection you lent me.
- p07-b-11-s05 · Nell: That could work. Its stories about families trying to save their farms should give us more to discuss than another crime plot.
- p07-b-11-s06 · Victor: Then let's choose The Last Orchard. We can each bring a question about our favourite story.
- p07-b-11-s07 · Nell: Agreed. I'll tell the group that's our book for next month.

## 用具体例子澄清草稿反馈的分歧 · p07-b-12

主题：工作与协作；情景：反馈与修改草稿（draft-feedback）。

沟通目标：提出可操作反馈并协商修订安排

背景：两名同事讨论客户提案草稿的反馈，准备修改而非争论个人能力。

人物：Ben、Priya。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-12-s01 · Ben: You sounded very critical of my proposal in the meeting. Is there anything you think I should keep?
- p07-b-12-s02 · Priya: Yes, the customer examples are strong. My criticism was about the opening, not the whole document.
- p07-b-12-s03 · Ben: Then our disagreement may be smaller than I thought. I wanted readers to understand the background first.
- p07-b-12-s04 · Priya: I understand. I don't want to criticize that aim, but they need to know what you're asking for before page three.
- p07-b-12-s05 · Ben: Would a short request at the top help, with the background underneath?
- p07-b-12-s06 · Priya: Yes. Keep the examples, but move the one about delivery closer to the request.
- p07-b-12-s07 · Ben: I'll revise the first two pages this afternoon. Could you read just those before I change the rest?
- p07-b-12-s08 · Priya: Of course. I'll mark any sentence where the request still isn't clear.

- critical · adj. · 批评的；指出问题的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8b852c63ee587bcf-p07-b-12；来源 ox-8b852c63ee587bcf；证据 p07-b-12-s01；词形 critical。
- criticism · n. · 批评意见；义项 sense-staged-ox-fc45fb9c0d6ea964-p07-b-12；来源 ox-fc45fb9c0d6ea964；证据 p07-b-12-s02；词形 criticism。
- disagreement · n. · 意见分歧；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c18cb9ea89dd28c-p07-b-12；来源 ox-1c18cb9ea89dd28c；证据 p07-b-12-s03；词形 disagreement。
- criticize · v. · 批评；指出不足；义项 sense-staged-ox-bbf71c73d3a40f98-p07-b-12；来源 ox-bbf71c73d3a40f98；证据 p07-b-12-s04；词形 criticize。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The apparent personal attack is narrowed to the draft opening, then converted into concrete changes: put the request first, retain examples, move the delivery example, and review the first two pages before revising further. The reused draft-feedback scene and Chinese title fit both the disagreement resolution and the negotiated revision arrangement.

## 缩小活动预约系统的首版交付范围 · p07-b-13

主题：工作与协作；情景：协调项目交付（project-delivery）。

沟通目标：澄清依赖、范围、风险与承诺

背景：虚构项目团队面对新增需求，协调活动预约系统的首版交付。

人物：Elise、Tariq。

- p07-b-13-s01 · Elise: Demand for the new booking system is higher than we expected. Can we add group bookings before Friday's delivery?
- p07-b-13-s02 · Tariq: That feature is dependent on a payment update we don't control. We can't promise it for Friday.
- p07-b-13-s03 · Elise: Individual bookings are crucial for the event launch. Could we release those first?
- p07-b-13-s04 · Tariq: Yes. The most efficient approach is to test that smaller version thoroughly instead of adding an unfinished feature.
- p07-b-13-s05 · Elise: Would one shared test sheet help the team work more efficiently? We keep repeating the same checks.
- p07-b-13-s06 · Tariq: It would. I'll assign each test once and record any failures there.
- p07-b-13-s07 · Elise: Then Friday's version will handle individual bookings only. I'll tell the organisers that group bookings have no confirmed date.
- p07-b-13-s08 · Tariq: Agreed. I'll flag any test failure that threatens even that smaller delivery.

- demand · n. · 对产品或服务的需求；义项 sense-staged-ox-ea40fbfaa2967d1d-p07-b-13；来源 ox-ea40fbfaa2967d1d；证据 p07-b-13-s01；词形 demand。

- efficiently · adv. · 高效地；义项 sense-staged-ox-88929492c01c1655-p07-b-13；来源 ox-88929492c01c1655；证据 p07-b-13-s05；词形 efficiently。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The group-booking dependency explains why the team narrows Friday’s release to individual bookings. They agree on a shared test sheet and explicit escalation of remaining test risks, while withholding a date for group bookings. The project-delivery taxonomy genuinely fits dependencies, scope, risk and commitment; the title accurately says the first-release scope is reduced.

## 了解河轮出发与订金要求后先索取条款 · p07-b-14

主题：出行与住宿；情景：核对河轮行程与订金后索取条款（river-cruise-deposit-inquiry）。

沟通目标：了解出发安排与交通限制并请求书面条款后再决定预订

背景：旅客克拉拉向虚构旅行社询问河轮旅行；报价与条件仅用于语言练习。

人物：Clara、Simon。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-14-s01 · Clara: I'm interested in the four-night river cruise. When does the boat depart?
- p07-b-14-s02 · Simon: At five on Monday afternoon. Passengers need to be on board by four.
- p07-b-14-s03 · Clara: My domestic flight arrives at three. That sounds too close.
- p07-b-14-s04 · Simon: I agree. You could arrive the previous day, but you'd need to arrange that separately.
- p07-b-14-s05 · Clara: Can passengers sit outside on the deck?
- p07-b-14-s06 · Simon: Yes, when it's open. There's an indoor lounge as well.
- p07-b-14-s07 · Clara: How much is the deposit, and what happens if I cancel?
- p07-b-14-s08 · Simon: The deposit is a hundred pounds per person. The refund conditions depend on when you cancel, so I'll send the full terms rather than summarize them from memory.
- p07-b-14-s09 · Clara: Please do. I'll check the flights and read the terms before deciding whether to book.
- p07-b-14-s10 · Simon: Certainly. Nothing has been reserved or charged.

- deposit · n. · 预付订金；义项 sense-staged-ox-17c191b6cd2a2536-p07-b-14；来源 ox-17c191b6cd2a2536；证据 p07-b-14-s07；词形 deposit。

The agent separates departure and boarding times, identifies the tight flight connection, explains deck access and deposit amount, and promises full cancellation terms rather than guessing them. The traveller ends by requesting terms before deciding, and the explicit no-reservation/no-charge statement matches the Chinese title and goal. Nothing falsely implies a completed booking.

## 因持续情绪低落请诊所安排专业回电 · p07-b-15

主题：健康与照护；情景：因持续情绪低落请求诊所回电（low-mood-clinic-callback）。

沟通目标：说明近期感受与咨询顾虑并请诊所安排专业人员回电

背景：虚构来电者向诊所接待员描述感受并求助。接待员不作诊断或治疗建议。

人物：Morgan、Receptionist。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-15-s01 · Morgan: I'd like to speak to someone about my mood. I've felt depressed for several weeks.
- p07-b-15-s02 · Receptionist: I can ask a clinician to call you. Is there anything you'd like me to include in the message?
- p07-b-15-s03 · Morgan: I haven't been sleeping well either. I wonder if I have a sleep disorder, but I don't know.
- p07-b-15-s04 · Receptionist: I'll record that as a concern for the clinician to discuss, not as a diagnosis.
- p07-b-15-s05 · Morgan: Thank you. Reading about depression online has been deeply upsetting. Some of the stories are quite depressing.
- p07-b-15-s06 · Receptionist: Would you like the clinician to know that you're worried about what you've read?
- p07-b-15-s07 · Morgan: Yes. I'm not expecting an instant cure. I just want help understanding what's happening.
- p07-b-15-s08 · Receptionist: I can include that. When can you answer a call?
- p07-b-15-s09 · Morgan: Any time after two today. I can take the call somewhere private.
- p07-b-15-s10 · Receptionist: I've sent the callback request with that time window. I can't promise an exact time, but the team will contact you to arrange the next step.

- depressed · adj. · 情绪低落的；沮丧的；义项 sense-staged-ox-1217536ec1ab925f-p07-b-15；来源 ox-1217536ec1ab925f；证据 p07-b-15-s01；词形 depressed。

- depression · n. · 抑郁症；抑郁状态这一健康问题；义项 sense-staged-ox-f78a08ed43cc1085-p07-b-15；来源 ox-f78a08ed43cc1085；证据 p07-b-15-s05；词形 depression。

- depressing · adj. · 令人沮丧的；义项 sense-staged-ox-33672141ef626e94-p07-b-15；来源 ox-33672141ef626e94；证据 p07-b-15-s05；词形 depressing。

## 拟定经济学项目的资料与观点采访问题 · p07-b-16

主题：学习与技能；情景：为经济学项目拟定采访问题（economics-project-interview-plan）。

沟通目标：把课程概念落实为资料来源和观点差异的采访问题

背景：学生路易斯与老师为课程项目准备采访一名虚构经济学家。

人物：Luis、Dr Hall。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-16-s01 · Luis: Does an interview fit the economics project, or do we have to write only about the textbook?
- p07-b-16-s02 · Dr Hall: The curriculum allows an interview, provided you connect it to our topic on prices.
- p07-b-16-s03 · Luis: A local economist has agreed to speak to me. I thought I'd ask whether prices will rise next year.
- p07-b-16-s04 · Dr Hall: You could, but also ask how they derive their forecasts. What data do they use?
- p07-b-16-s05 · Luis: And why their predictions might differ from another economist's?
- p07-b-16-s06 · Dr Hall: Exactly. That could help explain the debate rather than making the interview sound like a request for one certain answer.
- p07-b-16-s07 · Luis: Then I'll start with the sources, follow with the method, and finish with the reasons forecasts differ.
- p07-b-16-s08 · Dr Hall: Good. Send those questions in advance so your guest can bring an example you can discuss.

- economics · n. · 经济学；义项 sense-staged-ox-22d8ee616dca5127-p07-b-16；来源 ox-22d8ee616dca5127；证据 p07-b-16-s01；词形 economics。

## 把夸大的销售趋势说明改为实际下降幅度 · p07-b-17

主题：工作与协作；情景：修正销售图表的趋势说明（sales-chart-description-correction）。

沟通目标：核对点线和变化幅度，约定使用不过度夸张的图表说明

背景：两名同事核对虚构销量图：四个月销量依次为120、119、119和118件。

人物：Hana、Giles。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-17-s01 · Hana: Why does this chart look as if sales fell dramatically? They went from a hundred and twenty to a hundred and eighteen.
- p07-b-17-s02 · Giles: The vertical axis starts at a hundred and seventeen. That makes the downward curve look much steeper.
- p07-b-17-s03 · Hana: Did sales actually decrease in every month?
- p07-b-17-s04 · Giles: No. The middle two months were both a hundred and nineteen. That red dot marks the second of them.
- p07-b-17-s05 · Hana: Then the title 'A steep decline every month' isn't accurate.
- p07-b-17-s06 · Giles: Agreed. I'll replace it with 'Sales fell by two units over four months' and show the monthly figures.
- p07-b-17-s07 · Hana: Could you also show the full scale from zero so readers can judge the size of the change?
- p07-b-17-s08 · Giles: Yes. I'll keep the original values and change only the scale and the wording.

目标用法：

- dramatically · adv. · 显著地；幅度很大地；义项 sense-staged-ox-f127c7b8f523bbea-p07-b-17；来源 ox-f127c7b8f523bbea；证据 p07-b-17-s01；词形 dramatically。
- downward · adj. · 向下的；下降的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c678687340a7bd7d-p07-b-17；来源 ox-c678687340a7bd7d；证据 p07-b-17-s02；词形 downward。
- curve · n. · 图表中表示变化的曲线；义项 sense-staged-ox-bce8e840b5611f6a-p07-b-17；来源 ox-bce8e840b5611f6a；证据 p07-b-17-s02；词形 curve。
- decrease · v. · 减少；下降；义项 sense-staged-ox-372199ed06f95e7b-p07-b-17；来源 ox-372199ed06f95e7b；证据 p07-b-17-s03；词形 decrease。
- dot · n. · 小圆点；义项 sense-staged-ox-c6478e2953728c67-p07-b-17；来源 ox-c6478e2953728c67；证据 p07-b-17-s04；词形 dot。
- decline · n. · 下降；减少；义项 sense-staged-ox-3cab1fce5d64b02a-p07-b-17；来源 ox-3cab1fce5d64b02a；证据 p07-b-17-s05；词形 decline。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The four values in the context match the English figures: a two-unit net fall and a middle plateau. The speakers identify both an exaggerated scale and a false every-month label, agree on truthful wording, and preserve the values. The goal is explicitly agreement to revise, which the ending achieves. Targets inside the challenged label remain genuine lexical uses rather than endorsed factual claims.

- ox-c678687340a7bd7d：accepted；In s02, downward modifies curve as an adjective indicating the graph’s declining direction, not an adverb modifying starts.（p07-b-17-s02）。
- ox-bce8e840b5611f6a：accepted；In s02, curve is the plotted sales line whose apparent steepness depends on the vertical scale; it denotes a graph trace even with a flat middle section.（p07-b-17-s02）。

- ox-c6478e2953728c67：accepted；In s04, a red dot is the small round point marking one month’s value on the chart.（p07-b-17-s04）。

## 先练足球盯人和补位而不只盯比分 · p07-b-18

主题：文娱与运动；情景：针对失球原因调整足球防守练习（football-defence-practice-plan）。

- p07-b-18-s01 · Callum: Was Saturday's defeat mainly my fault? Their striker got past me twice.

- p07-b-18-s03 · Callum: I kept following the ball and lost track of the runner behind me.
- p07-b-18-s04 · Coach Evans: Then let's practise how to defend that space. One player closes down the ball while another watches the runner.
- p07-b-18-s05 · Callum: Can we try it slowly first? I want to know when to call for help.
- p07-b-18-s06 · Coach Evans: Yes. We'll walk through the movement before adding speed.
- p07-b-18-s07 · Callum: Good. I'd rather fix that than spend the session arguing about the score.

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

A player’s self-blame leads to a team-level explanation of missing cover and a practical slow walkthrough of tracking the runner and closing down the ball. The title and goal accurately describe a defensive practice plan, not a completed improvement. The British setting, defence and practise agree.

## 让篮球训练减少对单一得分手的依赖 · p07-b-19

主题：文娱与运动；情景：调整篮球队的传球与防守练习（basketball-team-play-adjustment）。

- p07-b-19-s01 · Maya: Our defense fell apart whenever Jordan came off the court.
- p07-b-19-s02 · Coach Reed: He's our dominant player, but the rest of the team needs to communicate without him.
- p07-b-19-s03 · Maya: He also seems to dominate every practice game. The others just pass to him and stop moving.
- p07-b-19-s04 · Coach Reed: Then we need a more dynamic practice, with everyone cutting into space and making decisions.
- p07-b-19-s05 · Maya: Could we start with a passing game where everyone has to touch the ball before a shot?
- p07-b-19-s06 · Coach Reed: Yes, then switch to a drill on defensive rotations. Jordan can join the second group rather than lead the first.
- p07-b-19-s07 · Maya: I'll explain the two drills and make sure everyone gets a turn.

- defense · n. · 球队的防守，美式拼写；义项 sense-staged-ox-9c63903b95d2192f-p07-b-19；来源 ox-9c63903b95d2192f；证据 p07-b-19-s01；词形 defense。

- dynamic · adj. · 充满活力并不断变化的；义项 sense-staged-ox-e689f2659dbe2f4c-p07-b-19；来源 ox-e689f2659dbe2f4c；证据 p07-b-19-s04；词形 dynamic。

The teammates connect overreliance on one player to defensive breakdown and static attacking play, then agree on an all-player passing game and defensive rotations. The final explanation of two drills is an achievable planned next step. US defense matches the declared setting, and dynamic is grounded in movement and distributed decisions.

## 先对照原始记录再判断报告差错 · p07-b-20

主题：工作与协作；情景：先核查报告差错再判断责任（report-error-fair-check）。

沟通目标：区分失误与故意行为，并约定对照原始记录核查

背景：两名同事发现内部报告数字不符，讨论如何公平查证，而不是未经证实给人定性。

人物：Zoe、Martin。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-20-s01 · Zoe: The totals in this report don't match the invoices. Do you think the change was deliberate?
- p07-b-20-s02 · Martin: I don't know. Calling someone dishonest before we check the records would be unfair.
- p07-b-20-s03 · Zoe: But we can't just dismiss the difference as a typing error.
- p07-b-20-s04 · Martin: I agree. I don't deny that it's a serious problem. I only question whether we know the cause.
- p07-b-20-s05 · Zoe: Then let's compare the original spreadsheet with the version used for the report.
- p07-b-20-s06 · Martin: I'll collect the invoices. Could you ask which version the figures came from?
- p07-b-20-s07 · Zoe: Yes. I'll ask about the process, without accusing anyone of changing the numbers on purpose.
- p07-b-20-s08 · Martin: Once we've checked, we can correct the report and explain what happened.

目标用法：

- deliberate · adj. · 故意的；有意为之的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0364df215a8408f2-p07-b-20；来源 ox-0364df215a8408f2；证据 p07-b-20-s01；词形 deliberate。
- dishonest · adj. · 不诚实的；义项 sense-staged-ox-552b84609c365b15-p07-b-20；来源 ox-552b84609c365b15；证据 p07-b-20-s02；词形 dishonest。
- dismiss · v. · 不予认真考虑；轻率排除；义项 sense-staged-ox-53484c74e46a4112-p07-b-20；来源 ox-53484c74e46a4112；证据 p07-b-20-s03；词形 dismiss。
- deny · v. · 否认某事属实；义项 sense-staged-ox-bcb6042e5229ba7d-p07-b-20；来源 ox-bcb6042e5229ba7d；证据 p07-b-20-s04；词形 deny。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The colleagues acknowledge a serious numerical discrepancy while separating facts from an accusation of intent. They assign invoice collection and version tracing before correction and explanation. The Chinese title and goal match this evidence-first plan, and the ending does not falsely claim the discrepancy is resolved.

- ox-552b84609c365b15：accepted；In s02, calling someone dishonest would label a person untruthful; the speaker refuses that unsupported character judgement.（p07-b-20-s02）。
- ox-53484c74e46a4112：accepted；In s03, dismiss the difference as a typing error means discounting it without adequate consideration, using the conventional object-plus-as construction.（p07-b-20-s03）。

## 为课堂代表选举安排发言与投票程序 · p07-b-21

主题：学习与技能；情景：设计课堂学生议会的选举程序（student-council-election-process）。

沟通目标：商定让不同学生表达意见并以投票选出代表的流程

背景：公民课堂中的师生为虚构班级代表选举讨论参与流程，不针对真实政治选举。

人物：Iris、Mr Patel。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-21-s01 · Iris: If this is an exercise in democracy, shouldn't everyone have a chance to speak before we elect a representative?
- p07-b-21-s02 · Mr Patel: Yes. What would make the process more democratic than simply choosing whoever speaks first?
- p07-b-21-s03 · Iris: We could hear from every candidate, then let everyone vote privately.
- p07-b-21-s04 · Mr Patel: That would give quieter students a way to express their choice too.
- p07-b-21-s05 · Iris: Our class is quite diverse, but the same three people usually lead every discussion.
- p07-b-21-s06 · Mr Patel: Then let's invite questions from everyone. We may hear a greater diversity of views if people can write their questions down.
- p07-b-21-s07 · Iris: I'll put out a question box. Can we give each candidate the same amount of speaking time?
- p07-b-21-s08 · Mr Patel: Agreed. Equal speaking time, written questions, and a private vote at the end. We'll explain that process before anyone stands.

目标用法：

- democracy · n. · 由成员参与决定或选举代表的民主方式；义项 sense-staged-ox-cab176f3e8e8e2ee-p07-b-21；来源 ox-cab176f3e8e8e2ee；证据 p07-b-21-s01；词形 democracy。
- elect · v. · 通过投票选举；义项 sense-staged-ox-fe5c56ac8fa3f929-p07-b-21；来源 ox-fe5c56ac8fa3f929；证据 p07-b-21-s01；词形 elect。
- democratic · adj. · 按民主参与原则进行的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2b20b884bc5dd1ad-p07-b-21；来源 ox-2b20b884bc5dd1ad；证据 p07-b-21-s02；词形 democratic。
- diverse · adj. · 多样的；由不同背景成员组成的；义项 sense-staged-ox-da204feef1b3d3ec-p07-b-21；来源 ox-da204feef1b3d3ec；证据 p07-b-21-s05；词形 diverse。
- diversity · n. · 多样性；义项 sense-staged-ox-5bc6ad1e47eb9659-p07-b-21；来源 ox-5bc6ad1e47eb9659；证据 p07-b-21-s06；词形 diversity。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The student and teacher establish equal candidate speaking time, written questions and a private vote before candidacy begins. The process promotes broader participation without asserting that an election has occurred. The classroom-civics context, title and procedural goal are coherent and not a real political campaign.

## 约定先演示拖放文件再发详细指南 · p07-b-23

主题：工作与协作；情景：安排软件拖放操作的演示（software-demo-request）。

沟通目标：确认屏幕及演示步骤，约定先演示再分发详细指南

背景：同事为一次软件培训安排屏幕和现场演示顺序。

人物：Evan、Sofia。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-23-s01 · Evan: Could you demonstrate how to move a file into a project folder? The written instructions confused a few people.

## 约定离婚后的陪伴不追问前伴侣 · p07-b-24

主题：家庭与人际；情景：离婚期间商定朋友陪伴的方式（divorce-friend-support-boundaries）。

沟通目标：表达支持和个人意愿并约定安静见面而不谈论前伴侣

背景：虚构朋友罗莎和贝丝谈论离婚后的情绪及陪伴边界。

人物：Rosa、Beth。

起草：author-phase07-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-b-24-s01 · Rosa: You've seemed distant lately. Have I done something to upset you?
- p07-b-24-s02 · Beth: No. Since the divorce, every conversation seems to turn into questions about my former husband.
- p07-b-24-s03 · Rosa: I'm sorry. I thought asking showed that I cared.
- p07-b-24-s04 · Beth: I know. I have no desire to discuss what went wrong tonight. I just miss spending ordinary time with you.
- p07-b-24-s05 · Rosa: I understand. You deserve some space without having to explain yourself.
- p07-b-24-s06 · Beth: It's been a disappointment that some friends only call when they want news.
- p07-b-24-s07 · Rosa: Would you like to come over for tea tomorrow? We can talk about something else, or not talk much at all.
- p07-b-24-s08 · Beth: Yes, that would be lovely. And please don't pass on anything about him unless I ask.
- p07-b-24-s09 · Rosa: I won't. Just tea and company tomorrow.
