- desire · n. · 想做某事的愿望；义项 sense-staged-ox-248dd0f66056e6d6-p07-b-24；来源 ox-248dd0f66056e6d6；证据 p07-b-24-s04；词形 desire。
- deserve · v. · 值得；应当得到；义项 sense-staged-ox-19cdd43c96fa0261-p07-b-24；来源 ox-19cdd43c96fa0261；证据 p07-b-24-s05；词形 deserve。
- disappointment · n. · 令人失望的事或体验；义项 sense-staged-ox-429f064f78f7e1ef-p07-b-24；来源 ox-429f064f78f7e1ef；证据 p07-b-24-s06；词形 disappointment。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The friends repair a misunderstanding about emotional distance and agree on tea and ordinary companionship without unsolicited news about the former husband. The support boundary is accepted explicitly at the end. The title, personal context and quiet-meeting goal fit; divorce remains background rather than an invitation to give legal advice.

- p07-b-25-s05 · Finn: I can distinguish the individual voices quite clearly. Now try the church recording.
- p07-b-25-s06 · Salma: There's more echo in that one. Does it make the words harder to hear?
- p07-b-25-s07 · Finn: A little. I'll borrow the studio recording because I want to learn the words.

The Chinese listening-comparison goal is achieved through distinguishable voices and echo, although 音质/清晰度 would be a more precise description than 音色; this wording does not change the actual choice.

- p07-b-31-s01 · Theo: Could I book an appointment? My right elbow has been hurting since yesterday.

- p07-b-31-s03 · Theo: Yes. Is that at the main clinic?
- p07-b-31-s04 · Receptionist: That's right, on Oak Street. Please arrive ten minutes early for the appointment.
- p07-b-31-s05 · Theo: Thank you. I'll be there at three twenty.

The receptionist explicitly confirms the booking, so the title and reused routine-clinic-booking goal are fully achieved without diagnosis or treatment advice.

## 了解大学电子制作入门工作坊的要求与费用 · p07-c-01

主题：学习与技能；情景：了解大学电子制作入门工作坊的要求与费用（electronics-workshop-access-inquiry）。

人物：Leila、Owen。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-01-s01 · Leila: I have an enquiry about the weekend electronics workshop. Is it open to people who are not students here?
- p07-c-01-s02 · Owen: Yes. The faculty runs it for the local community as well as our own students.
- p07-c-01-s03 · Leila: My knowledge is fairly elementary. I can change a battery, but that is about it.
- p07-c-01-s04 · Owen: That is enough to start. We equip each table with a simple kit, and a tutor demonstrates every step.
- p07-c-01-s05 · Leila: Will the kit enable me to build something that actually works?
- p07-c-01-s06 · Owen: You will make a small light. If you encounter a problem, the tutor can help you find it.
- p07-c-01-s07 · Leila: What is the fee, and do I need to buy any parts?
- p07-c-01-s08 · Owen: It is twenty pounds, with all parts included. Shall I email you the course outline?
- p07-c-01-s09 · Leila: Yes, please. I would like to read it before I register.
- p07-c-01-s10 · Owen: I will send it today. There is no need to reserve a place yet.

## 约定记录活动中心发电机的噪音与排放问题 · p07-c-03

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：约定记录活动中心发电机的噪音与排放问题（community-generator-complaint-record）。

沟通目标：区分噪音和排放问题，约定记录发生次数并交由维修团队评估

背景：居民反映活动中心备用发电机启动时的情况，中心经理准备交给维修团队核查。

人物：Ravi、Helen。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-03-s01 · Ravi: The power cuts are becoming more frequent, and the backup generator is right outside my window.
- p07-c-03-s02 · Helen: I understand. Is the noise the main problem, or have you noticed anything else?
- p07-c-03-s03 · Ravi: The noise is excessive, and there are strong fumes whenever it starts.
- p07-c-03-s04 · Helen: I will ask the maintenance team to check the emissions as well as the noise.
- p07-c-03-s05 · Ravi: Could they move it to the other side of the building?
- p07-c-03-s06 · Helen: Possibly, but moving it would not eliminate the noise. We need a proper assessment first.
- p07-c-03-s07 · Ravi: I can record the frequency of the starts. It ran four times yesterday.
- p07-c-03-s08 · Helen: That would help. We should also check why we need to generate our own electricity so often.
- p07-c-03-s09 · Ravi: I will send you the times from this week, without trying to inspect the machine myself.
- p07-c-03-s10 · Helen: Thank you. I will give that record to the maintenance team and ask for an assessment.

目标用法：

- frequent · adj. · 频繁的；义项 sense-staged-ox-df35c1985c331c42-p07-c-03；来源 ox-df35c1985c331c42；证据 p07-c-03-s01；词形 frequent。
- excessive · adj. · 过度的，超出合理程度的；义项 sense-staged-ox-dd9dd25aaae084e0-p07-c-03；来源 ox-dd9dd25aaae084e0；证据 p07-c-03-s03；词形 excessive。
- emission · n. · 排放出的气体；义项 sense-staged-ox-db63744910bfc6b0-p07-c-03；来源 ox-db63744910bfc6b0；证据 p07-c-03-s04；词形 emissions。
  - 词形依据：使用可数名词的复数 emissions，指发电机排放的气体。
- eliminate · v. · 消除；义项 sense-staged-ox-3a28b744682b1a9d-p07-c-03；来源 ox-3a28b744682b1a9d；证据 p07-c-03-s06；词形 eliminate。
- frequency · n. · 发生频率；义项 sense-staged-ox-bbdaaadc8af7b273-p07-c-03；来源 ox-bbdaaadc8af7b273；证据 p07-c-03-s07；词形 frequency。
- generate · v. · 发电，产生电能；义项 sense-staged-ox-028743e7b56338bd-p07-c-03；来源 ox-028743e7b56338bd；证据 p07-c-03-s08；词形 generate。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The resident distinguishes noise and fumes, proposes relocation and agrees to log starts, while the manager reserves technical judgment for maintenance. The community complaint goal accurately ends with a record and requested assessment, not a repair or a relocation promise.

## 为打破纪念碗道歉并约定先保留碎片 · p07-c-04

主题：家庭与人际；情景：为打破纪念碗道歉并约定先保留碎片（broken-keepsake-apology）。

沟通目标：承认损坏纪念物的责任，听取朋友感受并约定不擅自处理碎片

背景：托马斯打破了梅的祖母留下的碗，两人在厨房谈论这件事。

人物：Thomas、Mei。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-04-s01 · Thomas: I broke your grandmother's bowl while I was clearing the table. It was my fault.
- p07-c-04-s02 · Mei: I am furious. I told you that shelf was not safe for anything heavy.
- p07-c-04-s03 · Thomas: I was in a hurry, but that is not an excuse. I should have listened.
- p07-c-04-s04 · Mei: That bowl had a lot of emotional significance for me. A replacement would not be the same.
- p07-c-04-s05 · Thomas: I understand. I will not buy another one and pretend that fixes it.
- p07-c-04-s06 · Mei: Thank you. Sorting through her things has already been difficult emotionally.
- p07-c-04-s07 · Thomas: I hope you can forgive me eventually. Would you like me to put the pieces somewhere safe?
- p07-c-04-s08 · Mei: Yes, in that box, please. Do not try to repair them yet.
- p07-c-04-s09 · Thomas: I will leave any decision about the pieces to you.
- p07-c-04-s10 · Mei: That helps. I still have faith in you, but I need a little time to stop being upset.

- fault · n. · 过错，责任；义项 sense-staged-ox-0808ce094f757086-p07-c-04；来源 ox-0808ce094f757086；证据 p07-c-04-s01；词形 fault。

- excuse · n. · 为过错辩解的借口；义项 sense-staged-ox-88b5f86c844eb480-p07-c-04；来源 ox-88b5f86c844eb480；证据 p07-c-04-s03；词形 excuse。
- emotional · adj. · 感情上的；义项 sense-staged-ox-c47ded1303445d1b-p07-c-04；来源 ox-c47ded1303445d1b；证据 p07-c-04-s04；词形 emotional。
- emotionally · adv. · 在情感上；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef2037fe1802b933-p07-c-04；来源 ox-ef2037fe1802b933；证据 p07-c-04-s06；词形 emotionally。
- forgive · v. · 原谅；义项 sense-staged-ox-50fa79ecf3672309-p07-c-04；来源 ox-50fa79ecf3672309；证据 p07-c-04-s07；词形 forgive。

Thomas accepts responsibility and does not treat buying a replacement as a solution to sentimental loss. Mei permits boxing the pieces but not repair and remains upset. The relationship scene and Chinese goal accurately describe that limited agreement, not complete forgiveness.

## 把故事课开场改为先听故事再分享 · p07-c-05

主题：学习与技能；情景：把故事课开场改为先听故事再分享（storytelling-session-opening-revision）。

沟通目标：调整故事课的开场结构，先示范短故事再邀请学员自愿分享

背景：两名成人教育教师讨论下一次故事课的开场。

人物：Nora、Caleb。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-05-s01 · Nora: The opening talk is informative, but I am not sure it will engage people who have never told a story in public.
- p07-c-05-s02 · Caleb: Would an entertaining story work better than ten minutes of instructions?
- p07-c-05-s03 · Nora: Yes. I want the emphasis to be on sharing an experience, not giving a perfect performance.
- p07-c-05-s04 · Caleb: Then I will tell a short story about missing my bus. It has a clear beginning and a funny ending.
- p07-c-05-s05 · Nora: Could you also emphasize that nobody has to share before they feel ready?
- p07-c-05-s06 · Caleb: Of course. After my story, I will invite volunteers rather than call on people.
- p07-c-05-s07 · Nora: That sounds right. Let us move the technical advice to the end.
- p07-c-05-s08 · Caleb: Agreed. I will rewrite the opening around the story.

目标用法：

- engage · v. · 吸引并保持某人的兴趣；义项 sense-staged-ox-83ad161d0b47c791-p07-c-05；来源 ox-83ad161d0b47c791；证据 p07-c-05-s01；词形 engage。
- entertaining · adj. · 有趣的，使人愉快的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ef86eed0bac17d9d-p07-c-05；来源 ox-ef86eed0bac17d9d；证据 p07-c-05-s02；词形 entertaining。
- emphasis · n. · 重点；义项 sense-staged-ox-d46170ce2e475d2b-p07-c-05；来源 ox-d46170ce2e475d2b；证据 p07-c-05-s03；词形 emphasis。
- emphasize · v. · 强调；义项 sense-staged-ox-36eda69f5fd40310-p07-c-05；来源 ox-36eda69f5fd40310；证据 p07-c-05-s05；词形 emphasize。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Two teachers replace a technical lecture with a short model story and voluntary participation, postponing advice. Each suggestion responds to the concern about newcomers; the Chinese goal correctly reports an agreed revised structure rather than an already-delivered class.

## 决定继续试用简短版画廊语音导览 · p07-c-06

主题：文娱与运动；情景：决定继续试用简短版画廊语音导览（gallery-audio-trial-feedback）。

- p07-c-06-s01 · Sofia: Did the audio enhance your visit, or did it get in the way of looking at the paintings?
- p07-c-06-s02 · Martin: The short comments made it more enjoyable. The long biography was too much for me.
- p07-c-06-s03 · Sofia: The artist's enthusiasm really comes through in that recording. I was reluctant to cut it.
- p07-c-06-s04 · Martin: I can hear why. I am enthusiastic about hearing artists explain their work, just not for ten minutes beside one picture.
- p07-c-06-s05 · Sofia: So perhaps we should keep the short introduction and make the full interview optional.
- p07-c-06-s06 · Martin: Yes, and leave an easy way to turn the sound off.
- p07-c-06-s07 · Sofia: We will keep testing that shorter version this week.
- p07-c-06-s08 · Martin: I would be happy to try it again after the change.

## 限定帝国地图展品说明中的历史结论 · p07-c-07

主题：学习与技能；情景：限定帝国地图展品说明中的历史结论（empire-map-exhibit-wording）。

沟通目标：核对地图和旅行记摘录的证据范围，删去无法证明的控制范围与发现结论

背景：博物馆志愿者依据一组虚构档案材料核对展品标签，不讨论现实新闻。

人物：Inez、Peter。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-07-s01 · Inez: The label says this map proves the empire controlled every town shown. Is that what the source says?
- p07-c-07-s02 · Peter: No. It shows the routes used during that era, but a route is not the same as a border.
- p07-c-07-s03 · Inez: Then we can describe the routes, but not an expansion of trade unless we have earlier records to compare them with.
- p07-c-07-s04 · Peter: Exactly. This extract from a traveller's journal mentions markets along the route, but it does not tell us whether trade was growing.
- p07-c-07-s05 · Inez: Does it also prove the existence of the northern town marked with a question mark?
- p07-c-07-s06 · Peter: Not on its own. The traveller says someone told him about it; he did not visit.
- p07-c-07-s07 · Inez: Calling it an extraordinary discovery would overstate the evidence.
- p07-c-07-s08 · Peter: Yes. Let us make this exhibit about what the map and journal can, and cannot, tell us.
- p07-c-07-s09 · Inez: I will remove the claim about controlling every town and keep the uncertain place marked clearly.
- p07-c-07-s10 · Peter: I will add the journal reference beside the extract so visitors can see its source.

- empire · n. · 帝国；义项 sense-staged-ox-2fa5c164ae415bdd-p07-c-07；来源 ox-2fa5c164ae415bdd；证据 p07-c-07-s01；词形 empire。
- era · n. · 时代，历史时期；义项 sense-staged-ox-30fadbb36fdcd8f5-p07-c-07；来源 ox-30fadbb36fdcd8f5；证据 p07-c-07-s02；词形 era。
- expansion · n. · 扩展，扩大；义项 sense-staged-ox-a0b7ddbb9798805a-p07-c-07；来源 ox-a0b7ddbb9798805a；证据 p07-c-07-s03；词形 expansion。
- extract · n. · 摘录，节选；义项 sense-staged-ox-592b368299289387-p07-c-07；来源 ox-592b368299289387；证据 p07-c-07-s04；词形 extract。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Fully reread content_version 2, including every target and the entire ending. Revised s03 now explicitly withholds a trade-expansion claim without earlier comparative records, and s04 says market mentions do not show growth. This resolves the prior unsupported inference while preserving natural discussion. The volunteers also separate routes from borders and hearsay from direct evidence, then agree to remove the control claim, mark the uncertain place and cite the journal. The learning scene and Chinese evidence-limiting goal now match that precise outcome.

- p07-c-08-s01 · Ben: What made you decide to found a repair company rather than work for a large manufacturer?

- p07-c-08-s03 · Ben: Did you need an investor to finance the first workshop?
- p07-c-08-s04 · Amara: No. I used my own savings, started in a small rented room and bought very little equipment.

- p07-c-08-s06 · Amara: Yes, although I still spend more time fixing machines than talking about business.
- p07-c-08-s07 · Ben: For the article, should I call you the founder or the chief executive?
- p07-c-08-s08 · Amara: Founder is accurate. We now have a different chief executive who manages the company.
- p07-c-08-s09 · Ben: Good. I will identify you as the founder and explain that you still lead the repair team.
- p07-c-08-s10 · Amara: That describes my role well. Please send me the dates you want checked before publication.

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：删去口述历史报名表中的非必要身份问题（inclusive-history-workshop-signup）。

沟通目标：删去非必要的身份问题，并明确自愿分享、跳过问题及允许公开引用的边界

背景：社区口述历史活动的两名组织者审核报名表和参加说明。

人物：Tessa、Daniel。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-11-s01 · Tessa: This is a public history forum, so why does the registration form ask whether someone is gay?
- p07-c-11-s02 · Daniel: I copied an old form. It also asks about gender and ethnic background, but none of that is needed to book a seat.
- p07-c-11-s03 · Tessa: Let us remove those questions. People should have the freedom to decide which parts of their lives they share.
- p07-c-11-s04 · Daniel: Agreed. We should not exclude anyone who leaves the optional questions blank, either.
- p07-c-11-s05 · Tessa: I want people to speak freely during the discussion, without assuming that everything will appear online.
- p07-c-11-s06 · Daniel: Then we need a separate request for permission before using any personal story in the exhibition.
- p07-c-11-s07 · Tessa: That seems like the ethical approach. We must not exploit someone's difficult experience just because it makes a powerful display.
- p07-c-11-s08 · Daniel: I will add a clear choice: attend without sharing, share only in the room, or discuss permission for a public quotation later.
- p07-c-11-s09 · Tessa: Good. No public quotation should be approved just by signing up.
- p07-c-11-s10 · Daniel: I will remove the identity questions and bring the revised permission wording to our next meeting.

目标用法：

- forum · n. · 讨论会，交流论坛；义项 sense-staged-ox-a4f4f7b48d1d7832-p07-c-11；来源 ox-a4f4f7b48d1d7832；证据 p07-c-11-s01；词形 forum。
- gay · adj. · 同性恋的；义项 sense-staged-ox-73443c7e9cc5854a-p07-c-11；来源 ox-73443c7e9cc5854a；证据 p07-c-11-s01；词形 gay。
- gender · n. · 性别；义项 sense-staged-ox-6c0646f9cb6aa60b-p07-c-11；来源 ox-6c0646f9cb6aa60b；证据 p07-c-11-s02；词形 gender。
- ethnic · adj. · 族裔的；义项 sense-staged-ox-4197efe8df697c90-p07-c-11；来源 ox-4197efe8df697c90；证据 p07-c-11-s02；词形 ethnic。
- freedom · n. · 自行选择的自由；义项 sense-staged-ox-334fcf954922d6ac-p07-c-11；来源 ox-334fcf954922d6ac；证据 p07-c-11-s03；词形 freedom。
- exclude · v. · 将某人排除在外；义项 sense-staged-ox-a02991a9329741ce-p07-c-11；来源 ox-a02991a9329741ce；证据 p07-c-11-s04；词形 exclude。
- freely · adv. · 自由地，不拘束地；义项 sense-staged-ox-6784e81e233c7fd3-p07-c-11；来源 ox-6784e81e233c7fd3；证据 p07-c-11-s05；词形 freely。
- ethical · adj. · 符合道德原则的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f86186bc5d6a865e-p07-c-11；来源 ox-f86186bc5d6a865e；证据 p07-c-11-s07；词形 ethical。
- exploit · v. · 利用他人以谋求利益；义项 sense-staged-ox-6b1d9665cd73b585-p07-c-11；来源 ox-6b1d9665cd73b585；证据 p07-c-11-s07；词形 exploit。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The organizers identify intrusive inherited form questions and separate seat booking, private sharing and later permission for public quotation. The identity questions are used as examples of unnecessary collection, not stereotypes. The community scene and Chinese goal fit the agreed revision and permission boundaries; actual editing is still promised for the next meeting.

## 区分练习量与记忆效果并安排后续比较 · p07-c-12

主题：研究与论证；情景：区分词汇练习量与记忆效果（vocabulary-tool-activity-retention-comparison）。

沟通目标：区分完成题目数量与实际记忆效果，商定同等练习条件下的延后记忆测验和比较安排

背景：两名教师讨论一个虚构词汇练习工具的初步试用记录。

人物：Hugo、Beth。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-12-s01 · Hugo: How should we evaluate the new vocabulary tool? The pupils seem to like it, but enjoyment is not the same as learning.
- p07-c-12-s02 · Beth: We need a simple framework: time spent, questions attempted and what they remember a week later.
- p07-c-12-s03 · Hugo: Can the evaluation compare it with our usual paper exercises?
- p07-c-12-s04 · Beth: Yes, as long as both groups practise the same material for the same amount of time.
- p07-c-12-s05 · Hugo: One finding from the pilot is that pupils finished more questions. Can we say their vocabulary improved?
- p07-c-12-s06 · Beth: Not yet. It is evident from the log that they did more questions, but we have not checked what they remember.
- p07-c-12-s07 · Hugo: Then I will keep that result separate and add the follow-up check.
- p07-c-12-s08 · Beth: Good. We can review the comparison after both groups have taken it.

目标用法：

- evaluate · v. · 评价，评估；义项 sense-staged-ox-6144b49ada2db2d3-p07-c-12；来源 ox-6144b49ada2db2d3；证据 p07-c-12-s01；词形 evaluate。
- framework · n. · 评估框架，一组组织原则；义项 sense-staged-ox-287262813a785602-p07-c-12；来源 ox-287262813a785602；证据 p07-c-12-s02；词形 framework。
- evaluation · n. · 评估过程；义项 sense-staged-ox-ecac9ecc80a852b6-p07-c-12；来源 ox-ecac9ecc80a852b6；证据 p07-c-12-s03；词形 evaluation。
- finding · n. · 研究或调查发现；义项 sense-staged-ox-ff4b2d72f1468c3f-p07-c-12；来源 ox-ff4b2d72f1468c3f；证据 p07-c-12-s05；词形 finding。
- evident · adj. · 清楚可见的，明显的；义项 sense-staged-ox-66ed9a61739d8f8d-p07-c-12；来源 ox-66ed9a61739d8f8d；证据 p07-c-12-s06；词形 evident。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Fully reread content_version 2, all English turns and all five targets. The new title, scenario and goal now correctly distinguish question-completion activity from later vocabulary retention. Equal material and practice time are specified, a one-week recall check is proposed, and the speakers agree to review the comparison after testing rather than claim learning gains now. This resolves the former sample-size/generalization metadata mismatch; the reasoning scene and exact planned ending are aligned.

- ox-287262813a785602：accepted；In s02, a simple framework is an abstract organizing structure comprising time, attempted questions and later recall. These measures are appropriately linked to the revised activity-versus-retention goal.（p07-c-12-s02）。
- ox-ecac9ecc80a852b6：accepted；In s03, the evaluation is the planned assessment process that can compare the tool with paper exercises; s04 supplies matched practice conditions rather than reporting a result.（p07-c-12-s03）。

## 选定奇幻小说并安排读书小组讨论 · p07-c-13

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选择读书小组作品（reading-group-book-choice）。

沟通目标：比较故事题材与讨论安排后选定共读作品

人物：Lena、Oscar。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-13-s01 · Lena: Would the group prefer a realistic novel this month, or should we try a different genre?
- p07-c-13-s02 · Oscar: I would like some fantasy. What is The Clockmaker's Island about?
- p07-c-13-s03 · Lena: A young genius invents a machine that can stop time, but an evil ruler wants to use it against the islanders.
- p07-c-13-s04 · Oscar: Does the inventor become a famous hero?
- p07-c-13-s05 · Lena: Not really. She gives up the chance of fame so that nobody else can copy the machine.
- p07-c-13-s06 · Oscar: That gives us something to discuss beyond who wins the final battle.
- p07-c-13-s07 · Lena: The other book is another family drama. We have read three of those recently.
- p07-c-13-s08 · Oscar: Then let us choose The Clockmaker's Island. Can we discuss it on the last Thursday of the month?
- p07-c-13-s09 · Lena: Yes. I will ask everyone to read the whole book by then.
- p07-c-13-s10 · Oscar: I will prepare two questions about the inventor's final choice.

The book-group members compare fantasy with repeated family dramas, identify a moral choice worth discussing, select a fictional novel and set a Thursday discussion with preparation roles. ## 修订进化课堂海报中的个体与群体说法 · p07-c-14

主题：学习与技能；情景：修订进化课堂海报中的个体与群体说法（classroom-evolution-explanation-revision）。

沟通目标：区分个体学习与群体跨世代变化，修改海报中的进化说明及基因示例

背景：学生和生物老师检查课堂海报；这是基础概念交流，不涉及个人健康判断。

人物：Maya、Mr Reed。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-14-s01 · Maya: My poster says animals evolve because they try hard to adapt. Is that clear enough?
- p07-c-14-s02 · Mr Reed: It is clear, but misleading. Evolution describes changes in populations across generations, not an individual deciding to change.
- p07-c-14-s03 · Maya: So a bird learning a new song is not, by itself, an example of evolution?
- p07-c-14-s04 · Mr Reed: Right. That is a fundamental distinction between learning during one lifetime and inherited changes across generations.
- p07-c-14-s05 · Maya: Where should I explain what a gene is?
- p07-c-14-s06 · Mr Reed: Beside the DNA drawing. Say that a gene is a section of DNA, and use the example we studied in class.
- p07-c-14-s07 · Maya: Can I say every genetic difference changes the way an animal looks?
- p07-c-14-s08 · Mr Reed: No. Some differences have no visible effect. Avoid making the drawing stand for every possible change.
- p07-c-14-s09 · Maya: That fundamentally changes the claim in my first paragraph. I will rewrite it around populations and generations.
- p07-c-14-s10 · Mr Reed: Good. Bring the new version tomorrow and we will check that the example matches the explanation.

目标用法：

- evolve · v. · 逐步进化；义项 sense-staged-ox-5a3d51502b857d54-p07-c-14；来源 ox-5a3d51502b857d54；证据 p07-c-14-s01；词形 evolve。
- evolution · n. · 生物进化；义项 sense-staged-ox-f3ff2f5aa8472a42-p07-c-14；来源 ox-f3ff2f5aa8472a42；证据 p07-c-14-s02；词形 evolution。
- fundamental · adj. · 根本的，基础的；义项 sense-staged-ox-9af1b91c243cd7ff-p07-c-14；来源 ox-9af1b91c243cd7ff；证据 p07-c-14-s04；词形 fundamental。
- gene · n. · 基因；义项 sense-staged-ox-b623f64914c85b74-p07-c-14；来源 ox-b623f64914c85b74；证据 p07-c-14-s05；词形 gene。
- genetic · adj. · 基因的，遗传的；义项 sense-staged-ox-fc93a0af9b4987ee-p07-c-14；来源 ox-fc93a0af9b4987ee；证据 p07-c-14-s07；词形 genetic。
- fundamentally · adv. · 从根本上；义项 sense-staged-ox-9f7fbbc69955416d-p07-c-14；来源 ox-9f7fbbc69955416d；证据 p07-c-14-s09；词形 fundamentally。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The teacher explicitly corrects deliberate individual change as an account of biological evolution and separates learned behavior from inherited population change. The pupil also tests an overgeneralization about visible genetic effects and agrees to rewrite and return for checking. The learning scene and Chinese revision goal match a correction plan, without claiming the rewritten poster has already been approved.

- ox-f3ff2f5aa8472a42：accepted；In s02, Evolution is the sentence-initial abstract noun for biological population change across generations. Its ordinary capitalization preserves the lowercase declared form.（p07-c-14-s02）。
- ox-9af1b91c243cd7ff：accepted；In s04, a fundamental distinction is a foundational conceptual difference between lifetime learning and inherited change; the adjective is justified by the poster’s central misconception.（p07-c-14-s04）。
- ox-b623f64914c85b74：accepted；In s05, a gene is the singular count noun for a genetic unit; s06 links it to the DNA drawing and class example, making the biological sense explicit.（p07-c-14-s05）。
- ox-fc93a0af9b4987ee：accepted；In s07, genetic modifies difference and asks about variation related to genes. The teacher rejects the universal visible-effect claim, not the adjective’s meaning.（p07-c-14-s07）。
- ox-9f7fbbc69955416d：accepted；In s09, fundamentally changes modifies the verb and indicates a change to the claim’s basic reasoning, not a minor wording adjustment.（p07-c-14-s09）。

- p07-c-15-s01 · Jasper: Does this model show what happens before a volcano starts to erupt?
- p07-c-15-s02 · Alina: Yes. Watch the crack on the left. You can see red material begin to emerge there.
- p07-c-15-s03 · Jasper: I missed the change because I was writing. Can we watch it once without taking notes?
- p07-c-15-s04 · Alina: Good idea. Then we can replay it and record when the material first appears and when the main eruption begins.
- p07-c-15-s05 · Jasper: Let us do that. We should describe what the model shows, not pretend we observed a real volcano.
- p07-c-15-s06 · Alina: Agreed. I will put the name of the model at the top of our notes.

Two students solve the practical problem of missing a digital model’s sequence while taking notes: watch once, replay and record appearance and main-eruption times. ## 确定地质社团只观察并记录采石场样本 · p07-c-17

主题：自然与环境；情景：确定地质社团只观察并记录采石场样本（geology-club-observation-plan）。

沟通目标：选择可安全观察的岩层和疑似化石，约定拍照标注而不取走或过早确认样本

背景：地质社团成员在有公共步道的旧采石场讨论记录方法。

人物：Erin、Pavel。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-17-s01 · Erin: Is that curved mark in the rock a fossil?
- p07-c-17-s02 · Pavel: It might be, but we should not identify it from this distance. Take a photograph with the location noted.
- p07-c-17-s03 · Erin: There is a small fragment on the path too. Should I bring it to the meeting?
- p07-c-17-s04 · Pavel: Leave it here. Our exploration of the quarry does not include collecting samples.
- p07-c-17-s05 · Erin: Then I will photograph the larger rock formation from the viewing point.
- p07-c-17-s06 · Pavel: Good. We can compare its layers with the guidebook and ask our leader about the curved mark.
- p07-c-17-s07 · Erin: I will label the picture as unidentified rather than write a species name.
- p07-c-17-s08 · Pavel: That will give us a useful question for the meeting without claiming more than we know.

目标用法：

- fossil · n. · 化石；义项 sense-staged-ox-6cc170a086aec286-p07-c-17；来源 ox-6cc170a086aec286；证据 p07-c-17-s01；词形 fossil。
- fragment · n. · 碎片，小块；义项 sense-staged-ox-4c8e005a2a02294a-p07-c-17；来源 ox-4c8e005a2a02294a；证据 p07-c-17-s03；词形 fragment。
- exploration · n. · 探索，考察；义项 sense-staged-ox-929b0b07344efd3f-p07-c-17；来源 ox-929b0b07344efd3f；证据 p07-c-17-s04；词形 exploration。
- formation · n. · 自然形成的岩层或岩体；义项 sense-staged-ox-0595eccdd3db7e8d-p07-c-17；来源 ox-0595eccdd3db7e8d；证据 p07-c-17-s05；词形 formation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Club members avoid premature fossil identification and collecting, choosing photographs with location notes from a viewing point and later consultation. The nature scene, cautious title and goal all fit the actual observational outcome; no species or ownership claims are manufactured.

- ox-4c8e005a2a02294a：accepted；In s03, a small fragment denotes a detached piece of rock on the path, inferred naturally from the quarry setting; it is not an abstract textual fragment.（p07-c-17-s03）。
- ox-929b0b07344efd3f：accepted；In s04, our exploration of the quarry is a noun for the group’s investigative visit, explicitly bounded to observing rather than collecting samples.（p07-c-17-s04）。

## 预约检查屋顶渗漏和外墙损坏 · p07-c-18

主题：居家生活；情景：预约屋顶漏水检查（roof-leak-inspection-appointment）。

沟通目标：说明漏水情况及已采取的隔离措施，约定检查时间并在检查后讨论维修选项

背景：租户向房屋维护人员描述雨后漏水情况，安排检查而不是自行处理。

人物：Grace、Leon。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-18-s01 · Grace: Water came through the bedroom ceiling last night. There is also a damp patch on the external wall.
- p07-c-18-s02 · Leon: Is anyone using the room now?
- p07-c-18-s03 · Grace: No. I have closed it off and moved everyone to the other bedroom.
- p07-c-18-s04 · Leon: Thank you. We need a careful examination of the roof before deciding what to repair.
- p07-c-18-s05 · Grace: The marks look extensive, but I cannot tell how much damage is behind the paint.
- p07-c-18-s06 · Leon: Neither can I from a description. The inspector will assess the extent of the damage.
- p07-c-18-s07 · Grace: Can someone come tomorrow morning?
- p07-c-18-s08 · Leon: Yes, at nine. Please leave the room closed until the inspection.
- p07-c-18-s09 · Grace: That works. Will we discuss repair options afterwards?
- p07-c-18-s10 · Leon: Yes. Once we have the findings, I will call you to discuss the options.

目标用法：

- external · adj. · 外部的，外侧的；义项 sense-staged-ox-14b0e0ae4602f7d0-p07-c-18；来源 ox-14b0e0ae4602f7d0；证据 p07-c-18-s01；词形 external。

- extensive · adj. · 范围广的；义项 sense-staged-ox-b776ec78e2a0e235-p07-c-18；来源 ox-b776ec78e2a0e235；证据 p07-c-18-s05；词形 extensive。
- extent · n. · 范围，程度；义项 sense-staged-ox-e440752d41b26465-p07-c-18；来源 ox-e440752d41b26465；证据 p07-c-18-s06；词形 extent。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The tenant reports ceiling leakage and wall damp, confirms the room is unused and agrees to a nine-o’clock inspection tomorrow. Both speakers reserve conclusions about hidden damage and repair options until findings arrive. The reused home-inspection scene and Chinese title/goal fit the appointment and later discussion, not a diagnosis or completed repair.

- ox-b7487e9df8669c80：accepted；In s04, a careful examination of the roof is a noun for close inspection before deciding repairs, not an academic test.（p07-c-18-s04）。

## 根据失败原型的测试记录决定先查传感器 · p07-c-19

主题：工作与协作；情景：根据失败原型的测试记录决定先查传感器（prototype-failure-test-review）。

沟通目标：区分已测试项目和未测试条件，约定单独检查传感器后再改动原型

背景：两名产品开发同事复盘未能完成演示的原型。

人物：Niko、Faye。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-19-s01 · Niko: Are we keeping the failed prototype, or should I clear the bench?
- p07-c-19-s02 · Faye: Keep it. We need to understand the failure before we change anything.
- p07-c-19-s03 · Niko: I tested the motor extensively, so I assumed the whole system would work.
- p07-c-19-s04 · Faye: But the log shows the sensor stopped sending data when the room got dark. Did we test that condition?
- p07-c-19-s05 · Niko: No. All my tests were under the work lamp.
- p07-c-19-s06 · Faye: Then we can still gain something useful from this. Let us test the sensor separately in different light levels.
- p07-c-19-s07 · Niko: I will set that up and keep the original wiring unchanged.
- p07-c-19-s08 · Faye: Good. We can decide what to change after we have those results.

目标用法：

- failed · adj. · 未成功的，失败的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ecd30d51995cc4a3-p07-c-19；来源 ox-ecd30d51995cc4a3；证据 p07-c-19-s01；词形 failed。
- failure · n. · 失败，未达到预期结果；义项 sense-staged-ox-7ef01f14bdd3460f-p07-c-19；来源 ox-7ef01f14bdd3460f；证据 p07-c-19-s02；词形 failure。
- extensively · adv. · 广泛而充分地；义项 sense-staged-ox-49e09145180ad64a-p07-c-19；来源 ox-49e09145180ad64a；证据 p07-c-19-s03；词形 extensively。
- gain · v. · 获得有用的东西；义项 sense-staged-ox-d8ae1e296cd3aeef-p07-c-19；来源 ox-d8ae1e296cd3aeef；证据 p07-c-19-s06；词形 gain。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The colleagues avoid destroying a failed prototype, locate an untested lighting condition in the log and agree to isolate sensor testing before altering original wiring. The work scene and Chinese goal accurately describe the diagnostic next step, not a proved cause or repaired system.

绑定文件 SHA-256：b29627d11151dd898c1393dbb7e14df87f40cdc293f24945771bbedcb61c4ad8

- ox-ecd30d51995cc4a3：accepted；In s01, failed modifies prototype attributively as an unsuccessful design. It is a participial adjective describing the object’s state, not a finite past-tense predicate.（p07-c-19-s01）。
- ox-7ef01f14bdd3460f：accepted；In s02, understand the failure names the unsuccessful performance that needs explanation; the context is a failed demonstration, not a personal moral failing.（p07-c-19-s02）。
- ox-49e09145180ad64a：accepted；In s03, tested the motor extensively is an adverb of testing scope. The following discussion correctly shows that thorough motor testing did not establish whole-system behavior under an untested sensor condition.（p07-c-19-s03）。
- ox-d8ae1e296cd3aeef：accepted；In s06, gain something useful from this uses the transitive verb for obtaining knowledge or benefit from failure; the proposed isolated test makes the benefit concrete.（p07-c-19-s06）。

## 了解延期规则后决定按期归还预约书 · p07-c-20

主题：消费与服务；情景：了解延期规则后决定按期归还预约书（library-loan-extension-choice）。

沟通目标：澄清一般延期和预约书例外限制，决定按期归还当前书并保留阅读进度

背景：读者电话询问图书馆的虚构借阅规则。

人物：Wendy、Sam。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-20-s01 · Wendy: I have not finished the entire book. Could I get an extension on the loan?
- p07-c-20-s02 · Sam: Usually, yes. But someone has reserved this copy, so we cannot extend it.
- p07-c-20-s03 · Wendy: Could you make an exception for just two more days?
- p07-c-20-s04 · Sam: I am afraid not. For reserved books, the loan must not exceed the original period.
- p07-c-20-s05 · Wendy: I see. The delay is entirely my responsibility; I started it rather late.
- p07-c-20-s06 · Sam: You can place another request after you return it. Your reading notes might help you pick up where you left off.
- p07-c-20-s07 · Wendy: So, essentially, I need to return this copy tomorrow and wait my turn again.
- p07-c-20-s08 · Sam: That is right. I can show you how to make the new request when you bring it in.
- p07-c-20-s09 · Wendy: Thank you. I will note my page number and return it tomorrow.
- p07-c-20-s10 · Sam: You are welcome. We are open until six.

目标用法：

- entire · adj. · 整个的，全部的；义项 sense-staged-ox-d6a0d16c2cf448b6-p07-c-20；来源 ox-d6a0d16c2cf448b6；证据 p07-c-20-s01；词形 entire。
- extension · n. · 期限的延长；义项 sense-staged-ox-57a87c67254cdf93-p07-c-20；来源 ox-57a87c67254cdf93；证据 p07-c-20-s01；词形 extension。
- extend · v. · 延长期限；义项 sense-staged-ox-2612e7f539c7b9d0-p07-c-20；来源 ox-2612e7f539c7b9d0；证据 p07-c-20-s02；词形 extend。
- exception · n. · 例外，破例；义项 sense-staged-ox-12730c08073469ce-p07-c-20；来源 ox-12730c08073469ce；证据 p07-c-20-s03；词形 exception。
- exceed · v. · 超过规定限度；义项 sense-staged-ox-eae249c8d128f0bc-p07-c-20；来源 ox-eae249c8d128f0bc；证据 p07-c-20-s04；词形 exceed。
- entirely · adv. · 完全地；义项 sense-staged-ox-3b616d820798d7a5-p07-c-20；来源 ox-3b616d820798d7a5；证据 p07-c-20-s05；词形 entirely。
- essentially · adv. · 基本上，说到底；义项 sense-staged-ox-30f80fa79cf1d5d1-p07-c-20；来源 ox-30f80fa79cf1d5d1；证据 p07-c-20-s07；词形 essentially。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The borrower asks for extra time, learns the reservation restriction, accepts refusal and chooses to note her page and return tomorrow. The service conversation consistently distinguishes a possible future request from an extension now, and the Chinese title and goal match the precise on-time-return decision.

- ox-d6a0d16c2cf448b6：accepted；In s01, the entire book is an adjective phrase for the whole volume; not finished explains the request for more borrowing time.（p07-c-20-s01）。
- ox-57a87c67254cdf93：accepted；In s01, an extension on the loan is a count noun for additional borrowing time, immediately tested against the library’s reservation rule.（p07-c-20-s01）。
- ox-2612e7f539c7b9d0：accepted；In s02, cannot extend it uses a transitive verb whose pronoun refers to the loan discussed in s01; the intended extension is temporal, not physical.（p07-c-20-s02）。
- ox-12730c08073469ce：accepted；In s03, make an exception is the conventional noun collocation for setting aside the usual rule in this particular case.（p07-c-20-s03）。
- ox-eae249c8d128f0bc：accepted；In s04, must not exceed the original period uses the transitive verb for surpassing the allowed duration; loan is understood as its permitted borrowing term.（p07-c-20-s04）。

## 确定模型船承重实验的水量和记录方法 · p07-c-21

主题：学习与技能；情景：确定模型船承重实验的水量和记录方法（classroom-boat-load-test）。

沟通目标：核对水量单位，约定逐个增加相同砝码并记录模型船下沉情况

背景：两名学生使用教师准备的水箱和模型船规划课堂实验。

人物：Ava、Noah。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-21-s01 · Ava: The instruction sheet says to use one gallon of water. Is this jug the right size?
- p07-c-21-s02 · Noah: Yes. The jug and the sheet both say US gallon, so we are using the same measure.
- p07-c-21-s03 · Ava: Will the boat float with all six weights in it?
- p07-c-21-s04 · Noah: We do not know yet. Let us add one weight at a time rather than all six at once.
- p07-c-21-s05 · Ava: At the start, only a small fraction of the boat is below the water.
- p07-c-21-s06 · Noah: Take a photo of that first. We can record the number of weights and how low the boat sits after each step.
- p07-c-21-s07 · Ava: Good. We will use the identical weights the teacher gave us.
- p07-c-21-s08 · Noah: And keep the water level the same throughout the test.

目标用法：

- gallon · n. · 加仑，液体容量单位；义项 sense-staged-ox-e0f2963035981154-p07-c-21；来源 ox-e0f2963035981154；证据 p07-c-21-s01；词形 gallon。
- float · v. · 浮在水面上；义项 sense-staged-ox-2a016ecc570ab4bd-p07-c-21；来源 ox-2a016ecc570ab4bd；证据 p07-c-21-s03；词形 float。
- fraction · n. · 一小部分；义项 sense-staged-ox-80bb8194400390f1-p07-c-21；来源 ox-80bb8194400390f1；证据 p07-c-21-s05；词形 fraction。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The students resolve the gallon-standard ambiguity, choose identical weights added individually and plan photographs and load/depth records. The learning scene and Chinese goal correctly describe experiment planning, not a demonstrated load capacity. Keeping the tank water level fixed is an experimental control intention; the dialogue does not yet claim that results were obtained.

- ox-2a016ecc570ab4bd：accepted；In s03, will the boat float asks whether the loaded model will remain at the water surface; the intransitive verb contrasts with potential sinking.（p07-c-21-s03）。

- p07-c-22-s02 · June: It is lovely, but have you seen the price? It costs a fortune.
- p07-c-22-s03 · Isaac: Yes, it is well beyond my budget. I was fortunate to find Mina's wish list before I came; she also wants this little cactus.
- p07-c-22-s04 · June: That one is fifteen pounds, and it has the exact name she wrote down.
- p07-c-22-s05 · Isaac: Then I will take the cactus. I would rather give her something she asked for.
- p07-c-22-s06 · June: Good choice. I will leave the plant label attached and wrap the pot.

## 决定扫描旧照片并用信封避光存放 · p07-c-23

主题：家庭与人际；情景：决定扫描旧照片并用信封避光存放（family-photo-scanning-care）。

沟通目标：在拍摄和扫描之间选择扫描，约定避免强光并用合适信封保存旧照片

背景：兄妹整理家庭老照片，选择数字化和暂时存放的方式。

人物：Clara、Ethan。

起草：author-phase07-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p07-c-23-s01 · Clara: Could I use the camera flash to photograph these? The room is quite dark.
- p07-c-23-s02 · Ethan: It will probably reflect off the shiny surface. Let us use the scanner instead.
- p07-c-23-s03 · Clara: I was going to leave the photos on the sunny windowsill while we worked.
- p07-c-23-s04 · Ethan: Please do not expose them to direct sunlight. We can keep them on the table away from the window.
- p07-c-23-s05 · Clara: Then I will put each one in a separate envelope when we finish.
- p07-c-23-s06 · Ethan: Use these photo-storage envelopes. They will also reduce exposure to light while the photos are put away.
- p07-c-23-s07 · Clara: All right. I will scan them one at a time and write the names on the envelopes, not on the photographs.
- p07-c-23-s08 · Ethan: I will check the scans against the originals before we put everything away.

## 商定住宅区阅览室的六周试办安排 · p07-c-30

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：商定住宅区阅览室的六周试办安排（estate-reading-room-pilot-plan）。

审阅者：reviewer-phase07-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The residents choose an already-permitted laundry building for a six-week Saturday trial, select storable tables and plan feedback for residents who preferred a gym. The ending assigns confirmation of the table loan and preparation of the notice. The community scene and Chinese goal match an agreed pilot plan, not a permanent conversion or a completed opening.
